/**
 * Full-bleed cinematic backdrop. The artwork sits on the END side (left in RTL) and fades toward
 * the START side where the title block lives, then fades into the page background at the bottom.
 */
@Composable
internal fun PrimeBackdrop(details: FocusedPosterDetails?, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val rtl = LocalLayoutDirection.current == LayoutDirection.Rtl
    val bg = Palette.CinematicBg
    val compact = LocalDisplayMode.current == DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT
    val hasWideBackdrop = details != null &&
        details.backdropUrl.isNotBlank() &&
        details.backdropUrl != details.poster.image
    Box(modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        if (details != null) {
            val primaryImageUrl = if (hasWideBackdrop) details.backdropUrl else details.poster.image
            TrailerAutoPlayBox(
                trailerUrl = details.trailerUrl,
                posterUrl = primaryImageUrl,
                title = details.poster.name,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            )
        }
        val sideStops = if (rtl) {
            arrayOf(
                0.0f to Color.Transparent,
                0.42f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                0.70f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.78f),
                1.0f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.96f),
            )
        } else {
            arrayOf(
                0.0f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.96f),
                0.30f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.78f),
                0.58f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                1.0f to Color.Transparent,
            )
        }
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(
                    if (compact) {
                        Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(bg.copy(alpha = 0.30f), bg.copy(alpha = 0.75f)))
                    } else {
                        Brush.horizontalGradient(colorStops = sideStops)
                    },
                ),
        )
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colorStops = arrayOf(
                            0.0f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.55f),
                            0.18f to Color.Transparent,
                            0.52f to Color.Transparent,
                            0.78f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.72f),
                            1.0f to bg.copy(alpha = 0.94f),
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
        )
    }
}

internal val PrimeCyan = Color(0xFF00A8E1)

/** Title block: rank badge (1..10 only), big title, and real metadata line. */
@Composable
internal fun PrimeHeroInfo(
    details: FocusedPosterDetails?,
    rank: Int?,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val item = details ?: return
    val compact = LocalDisplayMode.current == DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT
    val validRank = rank?.takeIf { it in 1..10 }
    Column(modifier, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(if (compact) 3.dp else 4.dp)) {
        if (validRank != null) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                Text(
                    text = "#$validRank",
                    color = PrimeCyan,
                    fontSize = if (compact) 12.sp else 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
            }
        }
        Text(
            text = item.poster.name,
            color = Color.White,
            fontSize = if (compact) 19.sp else 24.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
        )
        val ratingValue = item.rating.toDoubleOrNull()
        val formattedRuntime = formatHeroRuntime(item.runtime)
        val realGenres = item.genre
            .split(",", "/", "،", "•")
            .map { it.trim() }
            .filter { it.isNotBlank() }
            .distinct()
            .joinToString("، ")
        val metaTail = listOf(
            item.year.trim(),
            realGenres,
            formattedRuntime.trim(),
        ).filter { it.isNotBlank() }.joinToString("  •  ")
        if (ratingValue != null || metaTail.isNotBlank()) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                if (ratingValue != null && ratingValue > 0.0) {
                    val filled = (ratingValue / 2.0).roundToInt().coerceIn(0, 5)
                    Text(
                        text = "★".repeat(filled) + "☆".repeat(5 - filled),
                        color = Palette.Gold,
                        fontSize = if (compact) 12.sp else 14.sp,
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = item.rating,
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = if (compact) 12.sp else 13.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    )
                }
                if (metaTail.isNotBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        text = metaTail,
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.85f),
                        fontSize = if (compact) 12.sp else 13.sp,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        if (item.plot.isNotBlank()) {
            Text(
                text = item.plot,
                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.88f),
                fontSize = if (compact) 12.sp else 14.sp,
                maxLines = 2,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            )
        }

    }
}

/** Formats raw IPTV duration strings (e.g. "01:25:23.648" or "105") into clean Arabic runtime text. */
internal fun formatHeroRuntime(raw: String): String {
    val clean = raw.trim().substringBefore('.').trim()
    if (clean.isBlank()) return ""
    val parts = clean.split(":")
    if (parts.size == 3) {
        val h = parts[0].toIntOrNull() ?: return clean
        val m = parts[1].toIntOrNull() ?: return clean
        return when {
            h > 0 && m > 0 -> "${h}س ${m}د"
            h > 0 -> "${h}س"
            m > 0 -> "${m}د"
            else -> ""
        }
    }
    if (parts.size == 2) {
        val m = parts[0].toIntOrNull() ?: return clean
        return if (m > 0) "${m}د" else ""
    }
    clean.toIntOrNull()?.let { mins ->
        val h = mins / 60
        val m = mins % 60
        return when {
            h > 0 && m > 0 -> "${h}س ${m}د"
            h > 0 -> "${h}س"
            m > 0 -> "${m}د"
            else -> ""
        }
    }
    return clean
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Spacer
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.drawWithCache
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Brush
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.LocalLightEffects
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.Palette
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.LocalThemePreferences

/** Deep cosmic background with cached glow / royal / blue nebulas (zero per-frame allocations). */
@Composable
fun DreamyBackground() {
    val light = LocalLightEffects.current
    val themeId = LocalThemePreferences.current.selectedThemeId
    val bgTop = Palette.BgTop
    val bg = Palette.Bg
    val glow = Palette.Glow
    val royal = Palette.Royal
    val blue = Palette.Blue
    Spacer(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .drawWithCache {
                val bgBrush = if (themeId == "oled_cinema") {
                    Brush.verticalGradient(colors = listOf(Color.Black, Color.Black))
                } else {
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(bgTop, bg, bg),
                        startY = 0f,
                        endY = size.height,
                    )
                }
                val nebulas = if (light || themeId == "oled_cinema") {
                    emptyList()
                } else {
                    val ambientScale = if (themeId == "dynamic_bento") 0.65f else 1f
                    listOf(
                        Triple(
                            Offset(size.width * 0.88f, size.height * -0.05f),
                            size.maxDimension * 0.55f,
                            Brush.radialGradient(
                                colors = listOf(glow.copy(alpha = 0.16f * ambientScale), glow.copy(alpha = 0.056f * ambientScale), Color.Transparent),
                                center = Offset(size.width * 0.88f, size.height * -0.05f),
                                radius = size.maxDimension * 0.55f,
                            ),
                        ),
                        Triple(
                            Offset(size.width * 0.75f, size.height * 0.10f),
                            size.maxDimension * 0.45f,
                            Brush.radialGradient(
                                colors = listOf(royal.copy(alpha = 0.12f * ambientScale), royal.copy(alpha = 0.042f * ambientScale), Color.Transparent),
                                center = Offset(size.width * 0.75f, size.height * 0.10f),
                                radius = size.maxDimension * 0.45f,
                            ),
                        ),
                        Triple(
                            Offset(size.width * 0.05f, size.height * 0.55f),
                            size.maxDimension * 0.42f,
                            Brush.radialGradient(
                                colors = listOf(royal.copy(alpha = 0.13f * ambientScale), royal.copy(alpha = 0.045f * ambientScale), Color.Transparent),
                                center = Offset(size.width * 0.05f, size.height * 0.55f),
                                radius = size.maxDimension * 0.42f,
                            ),
                        ),
                        Triple(
                            Offset(size.width * 0.65f, size.height * 1.02f),
                            size.maxDimension * 0.50f,
                            Brush.radialGradient(
                                colors = listOf(blue.copy(alpha = 0.12f * ambientScale), blue.copy(alpha = 0.042f * ambientScale), Color.Transparent),
                                center = Offset(size.width * 0.65f, size.height * 1.02f),
                                radius = size.maxDimension * 0.50f,
                            ),
                        ),
                    )
                }
                onDrawBehind {
                    drawRect(bgBrush)
                    for ((center, radius, brush) in nebulas) {
                        drawCircle(brush = brush, radius = radius, center = center)
                    }
                }
            },
    )
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement

/** The day's program guide for one channel. */
@Composable
fun EpgDialog(channelName: String, channelId: String, onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val repo = LocalContainer.current.repo
    var programs by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Program>?>(null) }
    var error by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var attempt by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }

    LaunchedEffect(channelId, attempt) {
        programs = null
        error = null
        try {
            programs = repo.dayEpg(channelId)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            error = e.userMessage()
        }
    }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss, properties = DialogProperties(usePlatformDefaultWidth = false)) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.92f)
                .fillMaxHeight(0.86f)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
                .background(Palette.DialogBg)
                .border(1.5.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.45f), RoundedCornerShape(28.dp)),
        ) {
            Row(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 22.dp, vertical = 16.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    DotTitle(Ar.epgTitle, Palette.Gold, fontSize = 14.sp)
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    Text(
                        channelName,
                        fontSize = 22.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Palette.Text,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                }
                GlassIconButton(Ic.Close, Ar.close, onDismiss, size = 48.dp, autoFocus = true)
            }

            val list = programs
            when {
                error != null -> MessageBox(error.orEmpty(), actionLabel = Ar.retry, onAction = { attempt++ })
                list == null -> LoadingBox()
                list.isEmpty() -> MessageBox(Ar.noGuide)
                else -> ProgramList(list)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ProgramList(programs: List<Program>) {
    val nowSec = remember { System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000 }
    val currentIndex = programs.indexOfFirst { nowSec in it.start until it.end }
    val listState = rememberLazyListState()
    LaunchedEffect(programs) {
        if (currentIndex > 0) listState.scrollToItem(currentIndex)
    }
    var expanded by remember { mutableStateOf<Int?>(null) }

    LazyColumn(
        state = listState,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
    ) {
        itemsIndexed(programs, key = { index, p -> "${p.start}_$index" }) { index, program ->
            val isNow = index == currentIndex
            val isPast = program.end <= nowSec
            val isOpen = expanded == index
            GlassCard(
                onClick = { expanded = if (isOpen) null else index },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                selected = isNow,
                focusScale = 1.015f,
            ) {
                Column(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Text(
                            text = "${formatClock(program.start)} - ${formatClock(program.end)}",
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            fontWeight = if (isNow) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                            color = if (isNow) Palette.Gold else Palette.TextDim,
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = program.title.ifBlank { Ar.noName },
                            fontSize = 17.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = if (isPast && !isNow) Palette.TextDim else Palette.Text,
                            maxLines = if (isOpen) Int.MAX_VALUE else 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        when {
                            isNow -> Tag(Ar.onAirNow, Palette.Live, filled = true)
                            isPast -> Tag(Ar.ended, Palette.TextDim)
                            else -> Tag(Ar.soon, Palette.Glow)
                        }
                    }
                    if (isNow) {
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                        val total = (program.end - program.start).coerceAtLeast(1)
                        ProgressLine(((nowSec - program.start).toFloat() / total), height = 4.dp)
                    }
                    if (program.description.isNotBlank()) {
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = program.description,
                            fontSize = 14.sp,
                            color = Palette.TextDim,
                            maxLines = if (isOpen) Int.MAX_VALUE else 2,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Semi-transparent dark input. The icon sits at the end of the field (left in RTL),
 * the border glows cyan when the field has focus.
 */
@Composable
fun GlassTextField(
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    inputModifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    placeholder: String = "",
    leading: ImageVector? = null,
    keyboardType: KeyboardType = KeyboardType.Text,
    imeAction: ImeAction = ImeAction.Done,
    password: Boolean = false,
    ltr: Boolean = false,
    onImeAction: () -> Unit = {},
) {
    val interaction = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val focused by interaction.collectIsFocusedAsState()
    val focusManager = LocalFocusManager.current
    val light = LocalLightEffects.current
    var reveal by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)

    val textStyle = TextStyle(
        color = Palette.Text,
        fontSize = 16.sp,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
        fontFamily = CairoFamily,
        textDirection = if (ltr) TextDirection.Ltr else TextDirection.Content,
        textAlign = if (ltr) TextAlign.Left else TextAlign.Start,
    )

    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .heightIn(min = 56.dp)
            .then(
                if (focused && !light) Modifier.shadow(14.dp, shape, ambientColor = Palette.Glow, spotColor = Palette.Glow)
                else Modifier,
            )
            .clip(shape)
            .background(Palette.InputBg)
            .border(
                width = if (focused) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                color = if (focused) Palette.Glow else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f),
                shape = shape,
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
        Box(Modifier.weight(1f), contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart) {
            if (value.isEmpty()) {
                Text(placeholder, fontSize = 15.sp, color = Palette.TextDim.copy(alpha = 0.7f), maxLines = 1)
            }
            BasicTextField(
                value = value,
                onValueChange = onValueChange,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .onPreviewKeyEvent { event ->
                        if (event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown) {
                            when (event.key) {
                                Key.DirectionUp -> focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Up)
                                Key.DirectionDown -> focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Down)
                                Key.DirectionLeft -> if (value.isEmpty()) focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Left) else false
                                Key.DirectionRight -> if (value.isEmpty()) focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Right) else false
                                else -> false
                            }
                        } else {
                            false
                        }
                    }
                    .then(inputModifier),
                singleLine = true,
                textStyle = textStyle,
                cursorBrush = SolidColor(Palette.Glow),
                interactionSource = interaction,
                visualTransformation = if (password && !reveal) PasswordVisualTransformation() else VisualTransformation.None,
                keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = keyboardType, imeAction = imeAction),
                keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                    onNext = {
                        if (!focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Next)) {
                            focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Down)
                        }
                    },
                    onDone = {
                        focusManager.clearFocus()
                        onImeAction()
                    },
                    onSearch = {
                        focusManager.clearFocus()
                        onImeAction()
                    },
                ),
            )
        }
        if (password) {
            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
            Text(
                text = if (reveal) Ar.hide else Ar.show,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = Palette.Glow,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                    .clickable { reveal = !reveal }
                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
            )
        }
        if (leading != null) {
            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            Icon(
                leading,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = if (focused) Palette.Glow else Palette.TextDim,
                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp),
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SearchBox(
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    placeholder: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    GlassTextField(
        value = value,
        onValueChange = onValueChange,
        modifier = modifier,
        placeholder = placeholder,
        leading = Ic.Search,
        imeAction = ImeAction.Search,
    )
}

/**
 * Sidebar navigation item of the identity: glass row, label on the start side, a count pill or icon
 * on the end side. When selected it gets the royal gradient, a cyan border and a glowing edge bar.
 */
@Composable
fun SideNavItem(
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    count: Int? = null,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    iconTint: Color = Palette.Glow,
    trailingText: String? = null,
    trailingColor: Color = Palette.Gold,
) {
    val isTv = com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.LocalIsTv.current
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
        selected = selected,
        focusScale = if (isTv) 1.02f else 1f,
    ) { focused ->
        if (selected && isTv) {
            Box(Modifier.matchParentSize()) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.CenterStart)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .width(5.dp)
                        .background(Palette.Glow),
                )
            }
        }
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .heightIn(min = if (isTv) 54.dp else 44.dp)
                .padding(horizontal = if (isTv) 18.dp else 12.dp, vertical = if (isTv) 12.dp else 8.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Text(
                text = label,
                fontSize = if (isTv) 16.sp else 13.5.sp,
                fontWeight = if (selected || focused) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                color = if (selected || focused) Palette.Text else Palette.TextDim,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            )
            if (trailingText != null) {
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Text(trailingText, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = trailingColor)
            }
            if (count != null) {
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                if (selected) {
                    Text(
                        count.toString(),
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Palette.Glow,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50))
                            .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.40f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 3.dp),
                    )
                } else {
                    Text(count.toString(), fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.TextDim.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
                }
            }
            if (icon != null) {
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, tint = iconTint, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Category picker: a glass sidebar on wide screens (televisions, tablets), a row of chips on phones.
 */
@Composable
fun CategoryPane(
    categories: List<Category>,
    selectedId: String?,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    wide: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    counts: Map<String, Int> = emptyMap(),
) {
    val listState = rememberLazyListState()
    if (wide) {
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = modifier.focusRestorer(),
            state = listState,
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 4.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            items(categories, key = { it.id }) { category ->
                SideNavItem(
                    label = category.name,
                    selected = category.id == selectedId,
                    onClick = { onSelect(category.id) },
                    count = counts[category.id]?.takeIf { it > 0 },
                )
            }
        }
    } else {
        LazyRow(
            modifier = modifier.focusRestorer(),
            state = listState,
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            items(categories, key = { it.id }) { category ->
                Chip(category.name, category.id == selectedId, { onSelect(category.id) })
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun HttpSecurityWarningBanner() {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(Palette.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.14f))
            .border(1.dp, Palette.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.45f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
        Icon(Ic.Warning, contentDescription = null, tint = Palette.Gold, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
        Text(
            text = "تنبيه: الاتصال عبر HTTP غير مشفّر. يُفضّل استخدام https:// إذا كان مزود الخدمة يدعمه.",
            fontSize = 13.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            color = Palette.Text,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
        )
    }
}

internal val IdleBorder get() = Palette.GlassBorder
internal val PrimaryBorder get() = Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.40f)
internal val IdleGlassFill: Brush get() = SolidColor(Palette.Glass)
internal val PhoneSelectedGlassFill: Brush get() = Brush.verticalGradient(
    listOf(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f), Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.20f)),
)
internal val PosterBottomShade: Brush get() = Brush.verticalGradient(
    listOf(Color.Transparent, Color.Transparent, Palette.DeepBlue.copy(alpha = 0.75f)),
)

/** Asks for keyboard/remote focus once, on televisions only. */
@Composable
fun Modifier.initialFocus(enabled: Boolean): Modifier {
    val requester = remember { FocusRequester() }
    val isTv = LocalIsTv.current
    if (enabled && isTv) {
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            withFrameNanos { }
            runCatching { requester.requestFocus() }
        }
    }
    return this.focusRequester(requester)
}

/**
 * The basic frosted-glass surface of the Sahab identity.
 * Idle: translucent dark glass with a ghost border.
 * Selected / focused: royal → deep-blue gradient with a glowing cyan border (glass-panel-active).
 * Focus also grows the card a little and adds a soft cyan glow, so it reads well from the sofa.
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun GlassCard(
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    shape: Shape? = null,
    onLongClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    selected: Boolean = false,
    fill: Brush? = null,
    autoFocus: Boolean = false,
    focusScale: Float = 1.04f,
    role: Role = Role.Button,
    contentDescription: String? = null,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.(focused: Boolean) -> Unit,
) {
    val resolvedShape = shape ?: RoundedCornerShape(LocalThemeCustomization.current.cornerRadius.dp)
    val interaction = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val focusedRaw by interaction.collectIsFocusedAsState()
    val isTv = LocalIsTv.current
    val focused = focusedRaw
    val pressed by interaction.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val light = LocalLightEffects.current

    val targetScale = when {
        pressed -> 0.97f
        focused && isTv -> focusScale
        else -> 1f
    }
    val spec: AnimationSpec<Float> = if (light) snap() else tween(140)
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(targetScale, spec, label = "glassScale")

    val background: Brush = when {
        fill != null -> fill
        focused -> Palette.ActiveFill
        selected -> if (isTv) Palette.ActiveFill else PhoneSelectedGlassFill
        else -> IdleGlassFill
    }
    val borderColor = when {
        focused -> Palette.FocusCyan
        selected -> if (isTv) Palette.FocusCyan.copy(alpha = 0.75f) else Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.75f)
        fill != null -> PrimaryBorder
        else -> IdleBorder
    }
    val borderWidth = if (focused) 2.5.dp else if (selected) 1.5.dp else 1.dp

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .initialFocus(autoFocus)
            .graphicsLayer {
                scaleX = scale
                scaleY = scale
            }
            .then(
                if (focused) {
                    Modifier.shadow(
                        elevation = 16.dp,
                        shape = resolvedShape,
                        ambientColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                        spotColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                    )
                } else Modifier,
            )
            .clip(resolvedShape)
            .background(background)
            .border(borderWidth, borderColor, resolvedShape)
            .semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {
                contentDescription?.let { this.contentDescription = it }
            }
            .combinedClickable(
                interactionSource = interaction,
                indication = null,
                role = role,
                onClick = onClick,
                onLongClick = onLongClick,
            ),
    ) {
        content(focused)
    }
}

/** A non-interactive glass panel (cards that hold other controls). */
@Composable
fun GlassPanel(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    shape: Shape? = null,
    strong: Boolean = false,
    padding: Dp = 18.dp,
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit,
) {
    val resolvedShape = shape ?: RoundedCornerShape(LocalThemeCustomization.current.cornerRadius.dp)
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(resolvedShape)
            .background(if (strong) Palette.GlassStrong else Palette.Glass)
            .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f), resolvedShape)
            .padding(padding),
        content = content,
    )
}

@Composable
fun GlassButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    primary: Boolean = false,
    autoFocus: Boolean = false,
    trailingIcon: ImageVector? = null,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier
            .heightIn(min = 54.dp)
            .then(
                if (primary) Modifier.shadow(14.dp, RoundedCornerShape(18.dp), ambientColor = Palette.Royal, spotColor = Palette.Royal)
                else Modifier,
            ),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
        fill = if (primary) Palette.Accent else null,
        autoFocus = autoFocus,
        role = Role.Button,
        contentDescription = text,
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.Center)
                .padding(horizontal = 26.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
        ) {
            if (icon != null) {
                Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, tint = Palette.Text, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            }
            Text(text, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text, maxLines = 1)
            if (trailingIcon != null) {
                Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                Icon(trailingIcon, contentDescription = null, tint = Palette.Text, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun GlassIconButton(
    icon: ImageVector,
    description: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 52.dp,
    selected: Boolean = false,
    tint: Color = Palette.Text,
    autoFocus: Boolean = false,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier.size(size),
        shape = CircleShape,
        selected = selected,
        autoFocus = autoFocus,
        focusScale = 1.08f,
        role = Role.Button,
        contentDescription = description,
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = description,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.Center)
                .size(size * 0.44f),
        )
    }
}

/** Pill-shaped selector (categories, seasons). */
@Composable
fun Chip(text: String, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { semSelected = selected },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(50),
        selected = selected,
        role = Role.Tab,
        contentDescription = text,
    ) {
        Text(
            text = text,
            fontSize = 15.sp,
            fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
            color = if (selected) Palette.Text else Palette.TextDim,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
        )
    }
}

/** Small coloured tag: "4K", "HD", "مباشر", ratings… */
@Composable
fun Tag(
    text: String,
    color: Color,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    filled: Boolean = false,
    fontSize: TextUnit = 11.sp,
) {
    Box(
        modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .background(if (filled) color else color.copy(alpha = 0.18f))
            .border(1.dp, color.copy(alpha = if (filled) 1f else 0.35f), RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 7.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
    ) {
        Text(text, fontSize = fontSize, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (filled) Color.White else color, maxLines = 1)
    }
}

/** A small coloured dot followed by a bold label, as used for section titles in the reference. */
@Composable
fun DotTitle(text: String, dot: Color, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, fontSize: TextUnit = 16.sp, color: Color = Palette.Text) {
    Row(modifier, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Box(Modifier.size(8.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(dot))
        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
        Text(text, fontSize = fontSize, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = color, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
    }
}

@Composable
fun ScreenHeader(
    title: String,
    onBack: (() -> Unit)?,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    trailing: @Composable RowScope.() -> Unit = {},
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
        if (onBack != null) {
            GlassIconButton(Ic.Back, com.saydlawy.sahab.util.Ar.back, onBack, size = 48.dp)
            Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
        }
        Text(
            text = title,
            style = TextStyle(brush = Palette.BrandText, fontSize = 26.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
        )
        trailing()
    }
}

@Composable
fun SectionTitle(text: String, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    DotTitle(text, Palette.Gold, modifier.padding(bottom = 12.dp), fontSize = 20.sp)
}

@Composable
fun LoadingBox(modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    Box(modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
        CircularProgressIndicator(color = Palette.Glow, trackColor = Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
    }
}

@Composable
fun MessageBox(
    message: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    actionLabel: String? = null,
    onAction: (() -> Unit)? = null,
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(32.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
    ) {
        Box(
            Modifier
                .size(64.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                .background(Palette.Glass)
                .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.3f), RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
        ) {
            Icon(Ic.Cloud, contentDescription = null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp))
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.size(16.dp))
        Text(
            text = message,
            fontSize = 17.sp,
            color = Palette.TextDim,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
        if (actionLabel != null && onAction != null) {
            Spacer(Modifier.size(18.dp))
            GlassButton(actionLabel, onAction, primary = true, autoFocus = true)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun RemoteImage(
    url: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    contentScale: ContentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
    fallbackText: String = "",
    fallbackUrl: String? = null,
    targetWidthPx: Int? = null,
    targetHeightPx: Int? = null,
) {
    val primaryUrl = url.trim().ifBlank { fallbackUrl?.trim().orEmpty() }
    val secondaryUrl = fallbackUrl?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() && it != primaryUrl }

    Box(modifier.background(Palette.CardSurface)) {
        if (primaryUrl.isNotBlank()) {
            val context = LocalContext.current
            var activeUrl by remember(primaryUrl, secondaryUrl) { mutableStateOf(primaryUrl) }
            var showFallback by remember(primaryUrl, secondaryUrl) { mutableStateOf(false) }
            if (showFallback) {
                FallbackLetter(fallbackText)
            }
            val request = remember<ImageRequest>(context, activeUrl, targetWidthPx, targetHeightPx) {
                ImageRequest.Builder(context)
                    .data(activeUrl)
                    .precision(Precision.INEXACT)
                    .apply {
                        if (targetWidthPx != null && targetHeightPx != null && targetWidthPx > 0 && targetHeightPx > 0) {
                            size(targetWidthPx, targetHeightPx)
                        }
                    }
                    .build()
            }
            AsyncImage(
                model = request,
                contentDescription = null,
                contentScale = contentScale,
                onSuccess = { showFallback = false },
                onError = {
                    if (secondaryUrl != null && activeUrl != secondaryUrl) {
                        activeUrl = secondaryUrl
                        showFallback = false
                    } else {
                        showFallback = true
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            )
        } else {
            FallbackLetter(fallbackText)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun FallbackLetter(text: String) {
    val letter = text.trim().firstOrNull()?.uppercaseChar() ?: return
    val gradient = remember(letter) {
        val palettes = listOf(
            listOf(Palette.Royal, Palette.Blue),
            listOf(Color(0xFF3B2A7A), Palette.Royal),
            listOf(Palette.Blue, Palette.Glow),
            listOf(Color(0xFF1E1B4B), Palette.Royal),
            listOf(Color(0xFF0F2A5C), Palette.Blue),
        )
        palettes[letter.code % palettes.size]
    }
    Box(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Brush.linearGradient(gradient)),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
    ) {
        Text(
            letter.toString(),
            fontSize = 22.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            color = Color.White,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun ProgressLine(fraction: Float, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, height: Dp = 4.dp) {
    Box(
        modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(height)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50))
            .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f)),
    ) {
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxHeight()
                .fillMaxWidth(fraction.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50))
                .background(Palette.Progress),
        )
    }
}

    private fun icon(name: String, path: String, autoMirror: Boolean = false): ImageVector {
        val builder = ImageVector.Builder(
            name = name,
            defaultWidth = 24.dp,
            defaultHeight = 24.dp,
            viewportWidth = 24f,
            viewportHeight = 24f,
            autoMirror = autoMirror,
        )
        // A malformed path must never crash the app; it just draws nothing.
        runCatching { builder.addPath(addPathNodes(path), fill = SolidColor(Color.Black)) }
        return builder.build()
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column

import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.DisplayMode

import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.Palette
import com.saydlawy.sahab.util.Ar

private data class ChromeTab(val route: String, val label: String, val icon: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector)

private val chromeTabs = listOf(
    ChromeTab(Routes.HOME, Ar.home, Ic.Home),
    ChromeTab(Routes.LIVE, "البث", Ic.Tv),
    ChromeTab(Routes.MOVIES, "الأفلام", Ic.Movie),
    ChromeTab(Routes.SERIES, "المسلسلات", Ic.Series),
    ChromeTab(Routes.FAVORITES, Ar.favorites, Ic.Heart),
)

@Composable
internal fun OledNavigationChrome(current: String?, onTab: (String) -> Unit, mode: DisplayMode) {
    if (mode == DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)).background(Palette.CardSurface)
                .border(1.dp, Palette.CardBorderIdle, RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)).padding(vertical = 8.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            chromeTabs.forEach { tab -> ChromeTabItem(tab, current, onTab, vertical = true) }
        }
    } else {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.width(76.dp).fillMaxHeight().padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 24.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)).background(Palette.CardSurface)
                .border(1.dp, Palette.CardBorderIdle, RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)).padding(vertical = 12.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
        ) {
            chromeTabs.forEach { tab -> ChromeTabItem(tab, current, onTab, vertical = false) }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ChromeTabItem(tab: ChromeTab, current: String?, onTab: (String) -> Unit, vertical: Boolean) {
    val selected = current == tab.route
    var focused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val bgColor = when {
        focused -> Palette.CardSurfaceElevated
        selected -> Palette.CardSurfaceElevated.copy(alpha = 0.7f)
        else -> Color.Transparent
    }
    val tint = if (focused || selected) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.TextMuted
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .background(bgColor)
            .then(
                if (focused) Modifier.border(1.5.dp, Palette.FocusCyan, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                else Modifier,
            )
            .onFocusChanged { focused = it.isFocused }
            .clickable { onTab(tab.route) }
            .padding(horizontal = 7.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp),
    ) {
        SahabIcon(tab.icon, tab.label, Modifier.size(if (vertical) 20.dp else 22.dp), tint)
        if (vertical) Text(tab.label, color = tint, fontSize = 9.sp, maxLines = 1)
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components

import androidx.compose.animation.core.AnimationSpec

@Composable
fun SkeletonGrid(
    columnWidth: Dp,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    itemCount: Int = 12,
) {
    val transition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "skeleton")
    val alpha by transition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.10f,
        targetValue = 0.24f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(durationMillis = 900, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse,
        ),
        label = "skeletonAlpha",
    )
    LazyVerticalGrid(
        columns = GridCells.Adaptive(columnWidth),
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp),
        userScrollEnabled = false,
    ) {
        items(itemCount, key = { it }) {
            Column {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .aspectRatio(2f / 3f)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = alpha)),
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth(0.7f)
                        .height(14.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                        .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = alpha * 0.8f)),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

/** Movie / series poster tile: glass frame, rating tag, optional quality tag and progress. */
@Composable
fun PosterCard(
    title: String,
    imageUrl: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    badge: String = "",
    onLongClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    contentScale: ContentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
    quality: String? = null,
    subtitle: String = "",
    progress: Float? = null,
    onFocused: (() -> Unit)? = null,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
        onLongClick = onLongClick,
        focusScale = 1.05f,
    ) { focused ->
        if (focused && onFocused != null) {
            LaunchedEffect(Unit) { onFocused() }
        }
        val isTv = LocalIsTv.current
        var posterSize by remember { mutableStateOf(IntSize.Zero) }
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .aspectRatio(2f / 3f)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(Palette.CardSurface)
                .onSizeChanged { posterSize = it },
        ) {
            RemoteImage(
                url = imageUrl,
                fallbackText = title,
                modifier = if (contentScale == ContentScale.Fit) Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(14.dp) else Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentScale = contentScale,
                targetWidthPx = posterSize.width.takeIf { it > 0 },
                targetHeightPx = posterSize.height.takeIf { it > 0 },
            )
            // Deep bottom gradient matching reference movie cards (simplified on TV for fast D-pad scroll)
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(
                        if (isTv) {
                            Brush.verticalGradient(
                                listOf(
                                    Color.Transparent,
                                    Palette.CinematicBg.copy(alpha = 0.45f),
                                    Palette.CinematicBg.copy(alpha = 0.95f),
                                ),
                            )
                        } else {
                            Brush.verticalGradient(
                                colorStops = arrayOf(
                                    0.0f to Color.Transparent,
                                    0.48f to Palette.CinematicBg.copy(alpha = 0.20f),
                                    0.76f to Palette.CinematicBg.copy(alpha = 0.80f),
                                    1.0f to Palette.CinematicBg.copy(alpha = 0.97f),
                                ),
                            )
                        },
                    ),
            )
            // Focused inner cyan rim highlight matching reference "سيد الظلام" card
            if (focused) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .border(2.5.dp, Palette.FocusCyan, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                )
            }

            // Bottom overlay content: Title, Subtitle, and Bottom Row (Star Rating on right, Quality pill on left)
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp),
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = title,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = Color.White,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
                if (subtitle.isNotBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        text = subtitle,
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        color = Palette.TextMuted,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                }
                if (badge.isNotBlank() || !quality.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    ) {
                        // Right side in RTL: Gold star rating
                        if (badge.isNotBlank()) {
                            Row(
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp),
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Ic.Star,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = Palette.Gold,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp),
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = badge,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                                    color = Palette.Gold,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                )
                            }
                        } else {
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(1.dp))
                        }

                        // Left side in RTL: Quality badge (4K / UHD / FHD / HD) - never collides with top-right poster logos!
                        if (!quality.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                    .background(Palette.CardSurfaceElevated.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
                                    .border(0.8.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.24f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 5.dp, vertical = 1.dp),
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = quality,
                                    fontSize = 9.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = if (quality == "4K" || quality == "UHD") Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (progress != null) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    ProgressLine(
                        progress,
                        Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        height = 3.dp,
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/** Guesses a quality tag from a title ("… 4K", "… FHD", "… HD"). */
fun qualityOf(name: String): String? {
    val u = name.uppercase()
    return when {
        "4K" in u || "UHD" in u -> "4K"
        "FHD" in u || "1080" in u -> "FHD"
        HD_REGEX.containsMatchIn(u) -> "HD"
        else -> null
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components

import androidx.compose.animation.AnimatedVisibility

import androidx.compose.animation.fadeOut

/**
 * Lightweight in-app banner shown while the catalog sync is running.
 * Intentionally NOT a system notification — just a small pill at the bottom.
 * Hidden automatically on the player screen to avoid distracting during playback.
 */
@Composable
fun SyncOverlay(visible: Boolean, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    AnimatedVisibility(
        visible = visible,
        enter = fadeIn() + slideInVertically { it / 2 },
        exit = fadeOut() + slideOutVertically { it / 2 },
        modifier = modifier,
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                .background(Palette.DialogBg)
                .border(1.dp, Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                .padding(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            CircularProgressIndicator(
                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp),
                strokeWidth = 2.dp,
                color = Palette.Royal,
            )
            Text(
                "جارٍ تحديث البيانات...",
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                color = Color.White,
            )
        }
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components

import androidx.compose.material3.Icon
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.drawBehind
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.drawscope.Stroke
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.graphicsLayer
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.LocalThemeCustomization
import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.theme.Palette

/** Shared icon entry point so theme scale and container treatment apply consistently. */
@Composable
fun SahabIcon(
    imageVector: ImageVector,
    contentDescription: String? = null,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    tint: Color = androidx.compose.material3.LocalContentColor.current,
) {
    val customization = LocalThemeCustomization.current
    val cornerRadius = if (customization.iconContainerShape == "square") 3.dp else 8.dp
    val styled = when (customization.iconStyle) {
        "filled" -> modifier.drawBehind {
            val radius = if (customization.iconContainerShape == "circle") size.minDimension / 2 else cornerRadius.toPx()
            drawRoundRect(color = Palette.Glass, cornerRadius = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.CornerRadius(radius))
        }
        "outlined" -> modifier.drawBehind {
            val radius = if (customization.iconContainerShape == "circle") size.minDimension / 2 else cornerRadius.toPx()
            drawRoundRect(color = Palette.GlassBorder, cornerRadius = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.CornerRadius(radius), style = Stroke(width = 1.dp.toPx()))
        }
        else -> modifier
    }
    Icon(
        imageVector = imageVector,
        contentDescription = contentDescription,
        modifier = styled.graphicsLayer {
            val scale = customization.iconScale.coerceIn(0.7f, 1.4f)
            scaleX = scale
            scaleY = scale
        },
        tint = tint,
    )
}

/** The rounded-square gradient tile with the Sahab cloud + play symbol. */
@Composable
fun BrandLogo(
    size: Dp = 44.dp,
    bright: Boolean = false,
    icon: ImageVector = Ic.Cloud,
) {
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(size * 0.28f)
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .size(size)
            .shadow(12.dp, shape, ambientColor = Palette.Royal, spotColor = Palette.FocusCyan)
            .clip(shape)
            .background(
                if (bright) {
                    Palette.LogoBright
                } else {
                    Brush.linearGradient(
                        colors = listOf(Color(0xFF3B82F6), Color(0xFF6366F1), Color(0xFF1D4ED8)),
                        start = Offset.Zero,
                        end = Offset(120f, 120f),
                    )
                },
            )
            .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.28f), shape),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
    ) {
        if (icon == Ic.Cloud) {
            Canvas(modifier = Modifier.size(size * 0.68f)) {
                val w = this.size.width
                val h = this.size.height
                val cloud = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(w * 0.25f, h * 0.72f)
                    lineTo(w * 0.76f, h * 0.72f)
                    cubicTo(w * 0.90f, h * 0.72f, w * 0.94f, h * 0.50f, w * 0.80f, h * 0.46f)
                    cubicTo(w * 0.78f, h * 0.26f, w * 0.52f, h * 0.22f, w * 0.44f, h * 0.38f)
                    cubicTo(w * 0.30f, h * 0.36f, w * 0.18f, h * 0.50f, w * 0.25f, h * 0.72f)
                    close()
                }
                drawPath(
                    path = cloud,
                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.95f),
                    style = Stroke(width = w * 0.075f, cap = StrokeCap.Round, join = StrokeJoin.Round),
                )
                val play = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(w * 0.46f, h * 0.43f)
                    lineTo(w * 0.46f, h * 0.65f)
                    lineTo(w * 0.65f, h * 0.54f)
                    close()
                }
                drawPath(play, color = Color.White)
            }
        } else {
            Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(size * 0.56f))
        }
    }
}

/** Logo + "سحاب" wordmark + "(Sahab IPTV)" caption matching reference layout while preserving Sahab identity. */
@Composable
fun BrandMark(
    subtitle: String? = "(Sahab IPTV)",
    logoSize: Dp = 42.dp,
    titleSize: Int = 20,
) {
    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        BrandLogo(logoSize)
        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy((-2).dp)) {
            Text(
                text = "سحاب",
                color = Color.White,
                fontSize = titleSize.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                maxLines = 1,
            )
            if (!subtitle.isNullOrBlank()) {
                Text(
                    text = subtitle,
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextMuted,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

/** Ticking "now" epoch millis value, refreshed every 30s. */
@Composable
fun rememberNow(periodMs: Long = 30_000L): Long {
    var now by remember { mutableStateOf(System.currentTimeMillis()) }
    LaunchedEffect(periodMs) {
        while (true) {
            delay(periodMs)
            now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        }
    }
    return now
}

/** Glass pill: "09:41 م • extra" (extra defaults to today's date). */
@Composable
fun ClockPill(extra: String? = null, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val now = rememberNow()
    val ar = Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")
    val time = formatNowMillis(now, "hh:mm a", ar)
    val trailing = extra ?: formatNowMillis(now, "EEEE، d MMMM", ar)
    Row(
        modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50))
            .background(Palette.Glass)
            .border(1.dp, Palette.GlassBorder, RoundedCornerShape(50))
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
        Text(time, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text, maxLines = 1)
        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
        Box(Modifier.size(6.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Palette.Glow))
        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
        Text(trailing, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = Palette.Glow, maxLines = 1)
    }
}

/**
 * The top bar shared by every main screen: brand, section tabs with glowing cyan underline,
 * live weather + clock, notification bell, search, and settings avatar button.
 */
@Composable
fun SahabTopBar(
    current: String?,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onBack: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    subtitle: String = "(Sahab IPTV)",
    showTabs: Boolean = true,
) {
    val onTab = LocalTabNavigator.current
    val displayMode = LocalDisplayMode.current
    val maxWidth = LocalWindowInfo.current.widthDp
    val customization = LocalThemeCustomization.current
    val adaptiveOledNavigation = LocalThemePreferences.current.selectedThemeId == "oled_cinema" &&
        displayMode != DisplayMode.PHONE_LANDSCAPE
    val showNavigation = showTabs && customization.isVisible("global_navigation") && !adaptiveOledNavigation
    val navigationPlacement = customization.componentPlacement["global_navigation"] ?: "center"

    Box(modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        when (displayMode) {
            DisplayMode.TABLET_OR_TV -> {
                val roomy = maxWidth >= 960.dp
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(start = 24.dp, end = 24.dp, top = 6.dp, bottom = 4.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    if (onBack != null) {
                        GlassIconButton(Ic.Back, Ar.back, onBack, size = 36.dp)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    }
                    BrandLogo(size = 36.dp)
                    if (showNavigation) {
                        if (navigationPlacement == "center" || navigationPlacement == "end") {
                            Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                        } else {
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(24.dp))
                        }
                        CinematicNavTabsRow(
                            current = current,
                            onTab = onTab,
                            compact = false,
                            scrollable = false,
                        )
                        if (navigationPlacement == "center") Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                        if (navigationPlacement == "start") Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    }
                    if (!showNavigation || navigationPlacement == "end") Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                    ) {
                        if (roomy) {
                            CompactTopBarInfo()
                        }
                        TopBarProfileButton(
                            active = current == Routes.SETTINGS,
                            onClick = { if (current != Routes.SETTINGS) onTab(Routes.SETTINGS) },
                            size = 38.dp,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            DisplayMode.PHONE_LANDSCAPE -> {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                ) {
                    if (onBack != null) {
                        GlassIconButton(Ic.Back, Ar.back, onBack, size = 36.dp)
                    }
                    BrandLogo(size = 32.dp)
                    if (showNavigation) {
                        CinematicNavTabsRow(
                            current = current,
                            onTab = onTab,
                            compact = true,
                            scrollable = true,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        )
                    } else {
                        Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    }
                    CompactTopBarInfo()
                    TopBarProfileButton(
                        active = current == Routes.SETTINGS,
                        onClick = { if (current != Routes.SETTINGS) onTab(Routes.SETTINGS) },
                        size = 34.dp,
                    )
                }
            }

            DisplayMode.PHONE_PORTRAIT -> {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(top = 6.dp, bottom = 4.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    ) {
                        if (onBack != null) {
                            GlassIconButton(Ic.Back, Ar.back, onBack, size = 36.dp)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        }
                        BrandMark(subtitle = "(Sahab IPTV)", logoSize = 34.dp, titleSize = 17)
                        Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                        ) {
                            CompactTopBarInfo()
                            TopBarProfileButton(
                                active = current == Routes.SETTINGS,
                                onClick = { if (current != Routes.SETTINGS) onTab(Routes.SETTINGS) },
                                size = 34.dp,
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    if (showNavigation) {
                        CinematicNavTabsRow(
                            current = current,
                            onTab = onTab,
                            compact = true,
                            scrollable = true,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 10.dp),
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
