@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
@Composable
internal fun PhonePortraitPlayerScreen(
    exo: ExoPlayer,
    item: PlayItem,
    playlist: List<PlayItem>,
    currentIndex: Int,
    isLive: Boolean,
    canSeek: Boolean = !isLive,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    buffering: Boolean,
    reconnecting: Boolean,
    error: String?,
    progressFlow: StateFlow<PlaybackProgress>,
    playbackSpeed: Float,
    tracks: Tracks,
    textOff: Boolean,
    hasVideoOverride: Boolean,
    currentProgram: Program?,
    nextProgram: Program?,
    seriesDetails: SeriesDetails?,
    controlsVisible: Boolean,
    resizeMode: Int,
    onToggleControls: () -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onTogglePlay: () -> Unit,
    onSeekBy: (Long) -> Unit,
    onSeekTo: (Long) -> Unit,
    onPrev: () -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
    onSelectPlaylistIndex: (Int) -> Unit,
    onSelectEpisode: (Episode) -> Unit,
    onCycleSpeed: () -> Unit,
    onCycleResize: () -> Unit,
    onOpenTracks: () -> Unit,
    onOpenGuide: () -> Unit,
    onEnterLandscape: () -> Unit,
    onRetry: () -> Unit,
) {
    val isInPipMode by SahabMediaSessionBridge.inPipMode.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    if (isInPipMode) {
        AndroidView(
            factory = { ctx ->
                PlayerView(ctx).apply {
                    useController = false
                    setShowBuffering(PlayerView.SHOW_BUFFERING_NEVER)
                    layoutParams = ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
                        ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                        ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                    )
                    player = exo
                }
            },
            update = { it.resizeMode = resizeMode },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(Color.Black),
        )
        return
    }

    val videoBadge = remember(tracks, item.title) {
        SahabPlayer.videoBadge(tracks, qualityOf(item.title) ?: "FHD")
    }
    val audioLabel = remember(tracks) { SahabPlayer.activeAudioLabel(tracks) }
    val qualityLabel = remember(tracks, hasVideoOverride) {
        SahabPlayer.activeQualityButtonLabel(tracks, hasVideoOverride, isLive)
    }

    val allEpisodes = seriesDetails?.episodes.orEmpty()
    val seasons = remember(seriesDetails) { seriesDetails?.seasons.orEmpty() }
    val currentEpObj = remember(allEpisodes, item.id) { allEpisodes.firstOrNull { it.id == item.id } }
    var selectedSeason by remember(currentEpObj, seasons) {
        mutableIntStateOf(currentEpObj?.season ?: seasons.firstOrNull() ?: 1)
    }
    val seasonEpisodes = remember(allEpisodes, selectedSeason) {
        if (allEpisodes.isEmpty()) emptyList() else allEpisodes.filter { it.season == selectedSeason }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(OsdSurfaceLowest),
    ) {
        // 1. Top 16:9 Video Viewport
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .aspectRatio(16f / 9f)
                .background(Color.Black),
        ) {
            AndroidView(
                factory = { ctx ->
                    PlayerView(ctx).apply {
                        useController = false
                        setShowBuffering(PlayerView.SHOW_BUFFERING_NEVER)
                        layoutParams = ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
                            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                        )
                        player = exo
                    }
                },
                update = { it.resizeMode = resizeMode },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            )

            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .pointerInput(Unit) {
                        detectTapGestures(onTap = { onToggleControls() })
                    },
            )

            if ((buffering || reconnecting) && error == null) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(
                    color = OsdCyan,
                    trackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f),
                    modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                )
            }

            // Touch Overlay inside 16:9 Video
            if (controlsVisible && error == null) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.48f))
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                ) {
                    // Top Row inside video: Back + Live/Quality + Fullscreen Landscape Button
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.TopCenter)
                            .fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    ) {
                        OsdIconSquareButton(
                            icon = Ic.Back,
                            description = Ar.back,
                            onClick = onBack,
                            size = 38.dp,
                            circle = true,
                        )
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        ) {
                            if (isLive) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50))
                                        .background(Palette.Live)
                                        .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 3.dp),
                                ) {
                                    Text(Ar.liveBadge, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                                }
                            }
                            FormatChip(videoBadge, OsdPrimary)
                            OsdIconSquareButton(
                                icon = Ic.Aspect,
                                description = Ar.fullscreen,
                                onClick = onEnterLandscape,
                                size = 38.dp,
                                circle = true,
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // Center Transport Row (LTR for standard media order)
                    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalLayoutDirection provides LayoutDirection.Ltr) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        ) {
                            if (playlist.size > 1) {
                                OsdIconSquareButton(Ic.Previous, Ar.previous, onPrev, size = 40.dp, circle = true)
                            }
                            if (canSeek) {
                                OsdIconSquareButton(Ic.Previous, "-10", { onSeekBy(-SEEK_STEP_MS) }, size = 42.dp, circle = true, label = "-10")
                            }
                            OsdPrimaryPlayButton(
                                isPlaying = isPlaying,
                                onClick = onTogglePlay,
                                size = 56.dp,
                                cornerRadius = 28.dp,
                            )
                            if (canSeek) {
                                OsdIconSquareButton(Ic.Next, "+10", { onSeekBy(SEEK_STEP_MS) }, size = 42.dp, circle = true, label = "+10")
                            }
                            if (playlist.size > 1) {
                                OsdIconSquareButton(Ic.Next, Ar.next, onNext, size = 40.dp, circle = true)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Bottom Scrubber inside video (isolated progress recomposition)
                    PhonePortraitTimelineSection(
                        progressFlow = progressFlow,
                        isLive = isLive,
                        canSeek = canSeek,
                        onSeekTo = onSeekTo,
                        onSeekBy = onSeekBy,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                            .fillMaxWidth(),
                    )
                }
            }

            error?.let { message ->
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.Center)
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                ) {
                    Text(message, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    GlassButton(Ar.retry, onRetry, primary = true)
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Channel / Title Metadata & Quick Action Toolbar (Below Video)
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .background(OsdSurfaceLow)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            ) {
                val primaryLogoUrl = remember(item.icon, item.title) {
                    ChannelLogoResolver.resolvePrimaryUrl(item.icon, item.title)
                }
                if (primaryLogoUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                    RemoteImage(
                        url = primaryLogoUrl,
                        fallbackText = item.title,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(46.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                            .background(OsdSurfaceHighest)
                            .padding(4.dp),
                        contentScale = if (isLive) ContentScale.Fit else ContentScale.Crop,
                    )
                }
                Column(Modifier.weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        item.title,
                        fontSize = 17.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Color.White,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                    val sub = if (isLive) {
                        currentProgram?.title?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                            ?: nextProgram?.let { "${Ar.nextShowing}: ${it.title}" }
                            ?: "بث مباشر فائق الوضوح"
                    } else {
                        item.subtitle.ifBlank { "مشاهدة مباشرة عبر مشغل سحاب Media3" }
                    }
                    Text(
                        sub,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        color = OsdPrimary,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                }
            }

            // Scrollable Action Pills Row (Never crowded!)
            LazyRow(
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 2.dp),
            ) {
                item {
                    OsdPillButton(
                        label = Ar.fullscreen,
                        leadingIcon = Ic.Aspect,
                        iconTint = OsdOnPrimary,
                        highlighted = true,
                        onClick = onEnterLandscape,
                    )
                }
                item {
                    OsdPillButton(
                        label = audioLabel,
                        leadingIcon = Ic.Equalizer,
                        iconTint = OsdCyan,
                        onClick = onOpenTracks,
                    )
                }
                item {
                    OsdPillButton(
                        label = qualityLabel,
                        leadingIcon = Ic.Quality,
                        iconTint = OsdPrimary,
                        onClick = onOpenTracks,
                    )
                }
                if (isLive) {
                    item {
                        OsdPillButton(
                            label = Ar.guide,
                            leadingIcon = Ic.Clock,
                            iconTint = OsdTertiary,
                            onClick = onOpenGuide,
                        )
                    }
                } else if (canSeek) {
                    item {
                        OsdPillButton(
                            label = String.format(Locale.US, "%.2fx", playbackSpeed).replace(".00x", ".0x"),
                            leadingIcon = Ic.Speed,
                            iconTint = OsdCyan,
                            onClick = onCycleSpeed,
                        )
                    }
                }
                item {
                    OsdPillButton(
                        label = "أبعاد الشاشة",
                        leadingIcon = Ic.Aspect,
                        iconTint = OsdOnSurfaceVariant,
                        onClick = onCycleResize,
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Vertical Channels or Episodes List (Takes remaining screen height without overlapping video)
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            if (isLive) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Text(
                        "دليل القنوات السريع",
                        fontSize = 16.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = OsdPrimary,
                    )
                    Text(
                        "${currentIndex + 1} / ${playlist.size}",
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = OsdCyan,
                    )
                }

                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 16.dp),
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(
                        playlist,
                        key = { idx, c -> "${c.id}_$idx" },
                        contentType = { _, _ -> "portrait_channel_row" },
                    ) { idx, ch ->
                        val active = idx == currentIndex
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                                .background(
                                    if (active) OsdPrimaryContainer.copy(alpha = 0.38f)
                                    else OsdSurfaceContainer.copy(alpha = 0.85f),
                                )
                                .border(
                                    width = if (active) 1.5.dp else 1.dp,
                                    color = if (active) OsdPrimary else OsdOutlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.30f),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                                )
                                .clickable { onSelectPlaylistIndex(idx) }
                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                String.format(Locale.US, "%02d", idx + 1),
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = if (active) OsdCyan else OsdOnSurfaceVariant,
                            )
                            RemoteImage(
                                url = ch.icon,
                                fallbackText = ch.title,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(40.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                    .background(OsdSurfaceHighest)
                                    .padding(3.dp),
                                contentScale = ContentScale.Fit,
                            )
                            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                Text(
                                    ch.title,
                                    fontSize = 15.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = if (active) Color.White else OsdOnSurface,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                )
                                Text(
                                    if (active && currentProgram != null) currentProgram.title else "بث مباشر",
                                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    color = if (active) OsdCyan else OsdOnSurfaceVariant,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                )
                            }
                            if (active) {
                                Icon(Ic.Equalizer, contentDescription = null, tint = OsdCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                            } else {
                                val q = qualityOf(ch.title) ?: "HD"
                                FormatChip(q, if (q == "4K") OsdPrimary else OsdOnSurfaceVariant)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Series Episodes or Movie Playlist in Portrait
                if (seasons.isNotEmpty()) {
                    LazyRow(
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 2.dp),
                    ) {
                        items(seasons, key = { it }) { sNum ->
                            val selected = sNum == selectedSeason
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(if (selected) OsdPrimary else OsdSurfaceHigh)
                                    .clickable { selectedSeason = sNum }
                                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    Ar.season(sNum),
                                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = if (selected) OsdOnPrimary else OsdOnSurface,
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 16.dp),
                ) {
                    if (seasonEpisodes.isNotEmpty()) {
                        itemsIndexed(seasonEpisodes, key = { _, ep -> ep.id }) { _, ep ->
                            val isCurrent = ep.id == item.id
                            val isWatched = currentEpObj != null &&
                                (ep.season < currentEpObj.season ||
                                    (ep.season == currentEpObj.season && ep.number < currentEpObj.number))
                            val isNext = currentEpObj != null &&
                                ep.season == currentEpObj.season &&
                                ep.number == currentEpObj.number + 1
                            val progState = if (isCurrent) progressFlow.collectAsStateWithLifecycle().value else null
                            val curPos = progState?.position ?: 0L
                            val curDur = progState?.duration ?: 0L
                            EpisodeDrawerCard(
                                number = ep.number,
                                title = ep.title,
                                plot = ep.plot.ifBlank { "حلقة كاملة من ${item.title}" },
                                imageUrl = ep.image.ifBlank { seriesDetails?.cover.orEmpty() }.ifBlank { item.icon },
                                durationText = ep.duration,
                                isCurrent = isCurrent,
                                isWatched = isWatched,
                                isNext = isNext,
                                progressFraction = if (isCurrent && curDur > 0L) (curPos.toFloat() / curDur).coerceIn(0.05f, 1f) else if (isWatched) 1f else 0f,
                                remainingMinutes = if (isCurrent && curDur > curPos) ((curDur - curPos) / 60_000L).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1) else 0,
                                onClick = { onSelectEpisode(ep) },
                            )
                        }
                    } else {
                        itemsIndexed(playlist, key = { i, p -> "${p.id}_$i" }) { idx, p ->
                            val isCurrent = idx == currentIndex
                            val isWatched = idx < currentIndex
                            val progState = if (isCurrent) progressFlow.collectAsStateWithLifecycle().value else null
                            val curPos = progState?.position ?: 0L
                            val curDur = progState?.duration ?: 0L
                            EpisodeDrawerCard(
                                number = idx + 1,
                                title = p.subtitle.ifBlank { p.title },
                                plot = p.title,
                                imageUrl = p.icon,
                                durationText = if (isCurrent && curDur > 0L) formatDuration(curDur) else "",
                                isCurrent = isCurrent,
                                isWatched = isWatched,
                                isNext = idx == currentIndex + 1,
                                progressFraction = if (isCurrent && curDur > 0L) (curPos.toFloat() / curDur).coerceIn(0.05f, 1f) else if (isWatched) 1f else 0f,
                                remainingMinutes = if (isCurrent && curDur > curPos) ((curDur - curPos) / 60_000L).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1) else 0,
                                onClick = { onSelectPlaylistIndex(idx) },
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// MODE B & C: UNIFIED FULLSCREEN OSD (Live TV, Movies, Series — Phone & TV)
// ============================================================================

@Composable
internal fun PhonePortraitTimelineSection(
    progressFlow: StateFlow<PlaybackProgress>,
    isLive: Boolean,
    canSeek: Boolean = !isLive,
    onSeekTo: (Long) -> Unit,
    onSeekBy: (Long) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val prog by progressFlow.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val position = prog.position
    val bufferedPosition = prog.bufferedPosition
    val duration = prog.duration
    Column(
        modifier = modifier,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp),
    ) {
        val progressFraction = if (duration > 0L) (position.toFloat() / duration).coerceIn(0f, 1f) else 0f
        val bufferedFraction = if (duration > 0L) (bufferedPosition.toFloat() / duration).coerceIn(progressFraction, 1f) else 0f
        GradientTimelineBar(
            progress = progressFraction,
            buffered = bufferedFraction,
            showChapters = false,
            onSeekToFraction = if (canSeek && duration > 0L) {
                { frac -> onSeekTo((duration * frac).toLong()) }
            } else null,
            onSeekBy = onSeekBy,
        )
        if (!isLive) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            ) {
                Text(formatDuration(position), fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                Text(formatDuration(duration), fontSize = 11.sp, color = OsdOnSurfaceVariant)
            }
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/player/PlayerLeaseCoordinator.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

/**
 * Coordinates exclusive ownership of active media players across preview and fullscreen surfaces
 * so that two [androidx.media3.exoplayer.ExoPlayer] instances never decode or hold live IPTV HTTP
 * connections simultaneously during screen transitions.
 */
enum class PlayerRole {
    PREVIEW,
    FULLSCREEN,
}

interface LeasedPlayerHandle {
    val role: PlayerRole
    fun stopAndRelease()
}

class PlayerLeaseCoordinator {

    private val lock = Any()
    private var activeHandle: LeasedPlayerHandle? = null
    private val releasedHandles = java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(
        java.util.IdentityHashMap<LeasedPlayerHandle, Boolean>(),
    )

    /**
     * Acquires the exclusive player lease for [handle].
     * Any previously active handle is synchronously stopped and released before [handle] becomes active.
     */
    fun acquire(handle: LeasedPlayerHandle) {
        val previous = synchronized(lock) {
            val prev = activeHandle
            activeHandle = handle
            if (prev != null && prev !== handle && !releasedHandles.contains(prev)) {
                releasedHandles.add(prev)
                prev
            } else {
                null
            }
        }
        previous?.runCatching { stopAndRelease() }
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops and releases [handle] if it has not already been released,
     * and clears the active lease if [handle] is the current holder.
     */
    fun release(handle: LeasedPlayerHandle?) {
        if (handle == null) return
        val shouldRelease = synchronized(lock) {
            if (activeHandle === handle) {
                activeHandle = null
            }
            releasedHandles.add(handle)
        }
        if (shouldRelease) {
            handle.runCatching { stopAndRelease() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops and releases the active handle if its role matches [role].
     */
    fun releaseByRole(role: PlayerRole) {
        val target = synchronized(lock) {
            val current = activeHandle
            if (current != null && current.role == role) {
                activeHandle = null
                if (releasedHandles.add(current)) current else null
            } else {
                null
            }
        }
        target?.runCatching { stopAndRelease() }
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops and releases whichever handle currently holds the lease.
     */
    fun releaseAll() {
        val target = synchronized(lock) {
            val current = activeHandle
            activeHandle = null
            if (current != null && releasedHandles.add(current)) current else null
        }
        target?.runCatching { stopAndRelease() }
    }

    fun activeRole(): PlayerRole? = synchronized(lock) {
        activeHandle?.role
    }

    fun isActive(handle: LeasedPlayerHandle): Boolean = synchronized(lock) {
        activeHandle === handle && !releasedHandles.contains(handle)
    }
}

// Design tokens from ExoPlayer/DESIGN.md & media-player/DESIGN.md
internal val OsdSurfaceLowest get() = Palette.color("OsdSurfaceLowest")
internal val OsdSurfaceLow get() = Palette.color("OsdSurfaceLow")
internal val OsdSurfaceContainer get() = Palette.color("OsdSurfaceContainer")
internal val OsdSurfaceHigh get() = Palette.color("OsdSurfaceHigh")
internal val OsdSurfaceHighest get() = Palette.color("OsdSurfaceHighest")
internal val OsdPrimary get() = Palette.color("OsdPrimary")
internal val OsdPrimaryContainer get() = Palette.color("OsdPrimaryContainer")
internal val OsdOnPrimary get() = Palette.color("OsdOnPrimary")
internal val OsdCyan get() = Palette.color("OsdCyan")
internal val OsdTertiary get() = Palette.color("OsdTertiary")
internal val OsdOnSurface get() = Palette.color("OsdOnSurface")
internal val OsdOnSurfaceVariant get() = Palette.color("OsdOnSurfaceVariant")
internal val OsdOutline get() = Palette.color("OsdOutline")
internal val OsdOutlineVariant get() = Palette.color("OsdOutlineVariant")

@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
private fun defaultResizeModes(): IntArray = intArrayOf(
    AspectRatioFrameLayout.RESIZE_MODE_FIT,
    AspectRatioFrameLayout.RESIZE_MODE_FILL,
    AspectRatioFrameLayout.RESIZE_MODE_ZOOM,
)

internal val ResizeModes = defaultResizeModes()

internal val PlaybackSpeeds = floatArrayOf(1.0f, 1.25f, 1.5f, 2.0f, 0.75f)

internal const val SEEK_STEP_MS = 10_000L
internal const val CONTROLS_HIDE_MS = 4_500L
internal val TimelineProgressBrush get() = Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(OsdPrimaryContainer, OsdPrimary, OsdCyan))

@Composable
fun PlayerScreen(container: AppContainer, onBack: () -> Unit) {
    val themeCustomization = LocalThemeCustomization.current
    val initialPlayback = remember { container.playback }
    var loadFinished by remember { mutableStateOf(initialPlayback != null) }
    var exited by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val request by produceState<PlayRequest?>(initialValue = initialPlayback, Unit) {
        if (initialPlayback != null) {
            value = initialPlayback
            loadFinished = true
        } else {
            value = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { container.restorePendingPlayback() }
            loadFinished = true
        }
    }
    if (loadFinished && (request == null || request!!.items.isEmpty())) {
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            if (!exited) {
                exited = true
                onBack()
            }
        }
        return
    }
    val resolvedRequest = request ?: return
    val exitPlayer: () -> Unit = {
        if (!exited) {
            exited = true
            container.clearPendingPlayback()
            onBack()
        }
    }
    PlayerContent(container, resolvedRequest, exitPlayer)
}

// Design tokens from ExoPlayer/DESIGN.md & media-player/DESIGN.md
@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
@Composable
internal fun TracksDialog(
    tracks: Tracks,
    exo: ExoPlayer,
    trackSelectionParams: TrackSelectionParameters = exo.trackSelectionParameters,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    val video = tracks.groups.filter { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO }
    val audio = tracks.groups.filter { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_AUDIO }
    val text = tracks.groups.filter { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT }
    val textOff = trackSelectionParams.disabledTrackTypes.contains(C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT)
    val hasVideoOverride = video.any { g -> trackSelectionParams.overrides.containsKey(g.mediaTrackGroup) }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(26.dp))
                .background(OsdSurfaceLow)
                .border(1.5.dp, OsdPrimary.copy(alpha = 0.45f), RoundedCornerShape(26.dp))
                .padding(20.dp),
        ) {
            Text(Ar.tracks, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = OsdOnSurface)
            Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
            LazyColumn(
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 4.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false),
            ) {
                if (video.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item { Text(Ar.videoQuality, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = OsdPrimary) }
                    item {
                        TrackRow(
                            label = Ar.qualityAuto,
                            selected = !hasVideoOverride,
                            onClick = {
                                exo.trackSelectionParameters = exo.trackSelectionParameters.buildUpon()
                                    .clearOverridesOfType(C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO)
                                    .build()
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                        )
                    }
                    itemsIndexed(
                        video.flatMap { group -> (0 until group.length).map { group to it } },
                        key = { idx, (group, i) -> "v_${group.mediaTrackGroup.hashCode()}_${i}_$idx" },
                    ) { _, (group, i) ->
                        TrackRow(
                            label = SahabPlayer.videoQualityLabel(group.getTrackFormat(i)),
                            selected = hasVideoOverride && group.isTrackSelected(i),
                            onClick = {
                                exo.trackSelectionParameters = exo.trackSelectionParameters.buildUpon()
                                    .setOverrideForType(TrackSelectionOverride(group.mediaTrackGroup, i))
                                    .build()
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (audio.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item { Text(Ar.audio, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = OsdCyan) }
                    itemsIndexed(
                        audio.flatMap { group -> (0 until group.length).map { group to it } },
                        key = { idx, (group, i) -> "a_${group.mediaTrackGroup.hashCode()}_${i}_$idx" },
                    ) { _, (group, i) ->
                        TrackRow(
                            label = trackLabel(group.getTrackFormat(i), i),
                            selected = group.isTrackSelected(i),
                            onClick = {
                                exo.trackSelectionParameters = exo.trackSelectionParameters.buildUpon()
                                    .setOverrideForType(TrackSelectionOverride(group.mediaTrackGroup, i))
                                    .build()
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (text.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item { Text(Ar.subtitles, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = OsdTertiary) }
                    item {
                        TrackRow(
                            label = Ar.subtitlesOff,
                            selected = textOff || text.none { g -> (0 until g.length).any { g.isTrackSelected(it) } },
                            onClick = {
                                exo.trackSelectionParameters = exo.trackSelectionParameters.buildUpon()
                                    .setTrackTypeDisabled(C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT, true)
                                    .build()
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                        )
                    }
                    itemsIndexed(
                        text.flatMap { group -> (0 until group.length).map { group to it } },
                        key = { idx, (group, i) -> "t_${group.mediaTrackGroup.hashCode()}_${i}_$idx" },
                    ) { _, (group, i) ->
                        TrackRow(
                            label = trackLabel(group.getTrackFormat(i), i),
                            selected = !textOff && group.isTrackSelected(i),
                            onClick = {
                                exo.trackSelectionParameters = exo.trackSelectionParameters.buildUpon()
                                    .setTrackTypeDisabled(C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT, false)
                                    .setOverrideForType(TrackSelectionOverride(group.mediaTrackGroup, i))
                                    .build()
                                onDismiss()
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
            GlassButton(Ar.close, onDismiss, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), autoFocus = true)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun TrackRow(label: String, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        selected = selected,
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            fontSize = 16.sp,
            fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
            color = OsdOnSurface,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        )
    }
}

internal fun trackLabel(format: Format, position: Int): String {
    val languageCode = format.language
    val language = if (languageCode.isNullOrBlank() || languageCode == "und") {
        null
    } else {
        val name = Locale.forLanguageTag(languageCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar"))
        name.ifBlank { languageCode }
    }
    return listOfNotNull(format.label, language).distinct().joinToString(" - ")
        .ifBlank { Ar.trackNumber(position + 1) }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/player/PlayerViewModel.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import androidx.media3.datasource.HttpDataSource
import com.saydlawy.sahab.AppContainer
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.Episode
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.Kind
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.PlayItem
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.PlayRequest
import com.saydlawy.sahab.data.SeriesDetails
import com.saydlawy.sahab.util.Ar
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable

/**
 * يستخرج رمز استجابة HTTP الفعلي عبر تتبع سلسلة `cause` لـ [HttpDataSource.InvalidResponseCodeException]
 * لتفادي التقاط أرقام موجودة داخل نص الرابط مثل `/403.mp4`.
 */
fun extractHttpResponseCode(throwable: Throwable?): Int? {
    var cur: Throwable? = throwable
    var depth = 0
    while (cur != null && depth < 12) {
        if (cur is HttpDataSource.InvalidResponseCodeException) {
            return cur.responseCode
        }
        cur = cur.cause
        depth++
    }
    return null
}

data class PlaybackProgress(
    val position: Long = 0L,
    val bufferedPosition: Long = 0L,
    val duration: Long = 0L,
)

@Immutable
data class PlayerUiState(
    val playlist: List<PlayItem>,
    val index: Int,
    val isPlaying: Boolean = false,
    val buffering: Boolean = true,
    val error: String? = null,
    val playbackSpeed: Float = 1.0f,
    val speedIndex: Int = 0,
    val resizeIndex: Int = 0,
    val retryKey: Int = 0,
    val formatAttempt: Int = 0,
    val autoReconnectCount: Int = 0,
    val reconnecting: Boolean = false,
    val seriesDetails: SeriesDetails? = null,
    val requestPlayFocusTick: Int = 0,
) {
    val currentItem: PlayItem
        get() = playlist.getOrElse(index.coerceAtLeast(0)) { PlayItem(Kind.LIVE, "", "") }
    val isLive: Boolean
        get() = currentItem.kind == Kind.LIVE
}

class PlayerViewModel(
    private val container: AppContainer,
    private val initialRequest: PlayRequest,
) : ViewModel() {

    private val repo = container.repo

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(
        PlayerUiState(
            playlist = initialRequest.items,
            index = initialRequest.startIndex.coerceIn(0, initialRequest.items.lastIndex.coerceAtLeast(0)),
        ),
    )
    val state: StateFlow<PlayerUiState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    private val _progress = MutableStateFlow(PlaybackProgress())
    val progress: StateFlow<PlaybackProgress> = _progress.asStateFlow()

    private var forceFromZeroId: String? = null
    private var usedInitialPos: Boolean = false
    private var lastLoadedSeriesGroupId: String? = null
    private var retryJob: Job? = null

    fun saveProgress(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        val current = _state.value.currentItem
        if (current.id.isBlank()) return
        container.saveProgress(current, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun saveProgressIfVod(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        val current = _state.value.currentItem
        if (current.id.isBlank() || current.kind == Kind.LIVE) return
        container.saveProgress(current, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun flushRecents() {
        container.flushRecents()
    }

    fun onPlaybackPausedOrStopped(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        saveProgress(positionMs, durationMs)
        container.flushRecents()
    }

    fun loadSeriesIfNeeded(item: PlayItem) {
        if (item.kind != Kind.EPISODE || item.groupId.isBlank()) return
        if (lastLoadedSeriesGroupId == item.groupId && _state.value.seriesDetails != null) return
        lastLoadedSeriesGroupId = item.groupId
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val loaded = runCatching { repo.seriesDetails(item.groupId) }.getOrNull() ?: return@launch
            _state.update { s ->
                val expanded = if (loaded.episodes.isNotEmpty() && s.playlist.size <= 1) {
                    loaded.episodes.map { e ->
                        PlayItem(
                            kind = Kind.EPISODE,
                            id = e.id,
                            title = loaded.name.ifBlank { item.title },
                            subtitle = "${Ar.seasonEpisode(e.season, e.number)}: ${e.title}",
                            icon = e.image.ifBlank { loaded.cover.ifBlank { item.icon } },
                            ext = e.ext,
                            groupId = loaded.id.ifBlank { item.groupId },
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    s.playlist
                }
                val matchIdx = expanded.indexOfFirst { it.id == s.currentItem.id }
                s.copy(
                    seriesDetails = loaded,
                    playlist = expanded,
                    index = if (matchIdx >= 0) matchIdx else s.index,
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun goTo(target: Int, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val current = _state.value
        if (target !in 0..current.playlist.lastIndex || target == current.index) return false
        retryJob?.cancel()
        saveProgress(positionMs, durationMs)
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                formatAttempt = 0,
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
                index = target,
            )
        }
        return true
    }

    fun zapNext(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        if (s.playlist.size <= 1) return false
        val target = if (s.index >= s.playlist.lastIndex) 0 else s.index + 1
        return goTo(target, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun zapPrevious(positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        if (s.playlist.size <= 1) return false
        val target = if (s.index <= 0) s.playlist.lastIndex else s.index - 1
        return goTo(target, positionMs, durationMs)
    }

    fun playEpisodeFromDrawer(ep: Episode, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        val existingIdx = s.playlist.indexOfFirst { it.id == ep.id }
        if (existingIdx >= 0) {
            return goTo(existingIdx, positionMs, durationMs)
        }
        val details = s.seriesDetails ?: return false
        val allEps = details.episodes
        if (allEps.isEmpty()) return false
        val sName = details.name.ifBlank { s.currentItem.title }
        val sCover = details.cover.ifBlank { s.currentItem.icon }
        val sId = details.id.ifBlank { s.currentItem.groupId }
        val newItems = allEps.map { e ->
            PlayItem(
                kind = Kind.EPISODE,
                id = e.id,
                title = sName,
                subtitle = "${Ar.seasonEpisode(e.season, e.number)}: ${e.title}",
                icon = e.image.ifBlank { sCover },
                ext = e.ext,
                groupId = sId,
            )
        }
        val newIdx = newItems.indexOfFirst { it.id == ep.id }.coerceAtLeast(0)
        saveProgress(positionMs, durationMs)
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                playlist = newItems,
                formatAttempt = 0,
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
                index = newIdx,
            )
        }
        return true
    }

    fun cycleSpeed(speeds: FloatArray): Float {
        val nextIdx = (_state.value.speedIndex + 1) % speeds.size
        val nextSpeed = speeds[nextIdx]
        _state.update { it.copy(speedIndex = nextIdx, playbackSpeed = nextSpeed) }
        return nextSpeed
    }

    fun cycleResize(modeCount: Int) {
        _state.update { it.copy(resizeIndex = (it.resizeIndex + 1) % modeCount) }
    }

    fun retry() {
        retryJob?.cancel()
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                formatAttempt = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
                retryKey = it.retryKey + 1,
            )
        }
    }

    fun bumpPlayFocusTick() {
        _state.update { it.copy(requestPlayFocusTick = it.requestPlayFocusTick + 1) }
    }

    fun onIsPlayingChanged(playing: Boolean) {
        _state.update { it.copy(isPlaying = playing) }
    }

    fun onProgressUpdate(position: Long, buffered: Long, duration: Long) {
        _progress.value = PlaybackProgress(position, buffered, duration)
    }

    fun onSeekPosition(position: Long) {
        _progress.update { it.copy(position = position) }
    }

    fun onBufferingReady(isBuffering: Boolean, isReady: Boolean) {
        _state.update { s ->
            if (isReady) {
                s.copy(
                    buffering = isBuffering,
                    error = null,
                    reconnecting = false,
                    autoReconnectCount = 0,
                )
            } else {
                s.copy(buffering = isBuffering)
            }
        }
    }

    fun onPrepareStarted() {
        _state.update { it.copy(error = null, buffering = true) }
    }

    fun onUrlError() {
        _state.update { it.copy(reconnecting = false, error = Ar.errPlayback) }
    }

    fun onPlayerError(errorCodeName: String, responseCode: Int? = null) {
        retryJob?.cancel()

        val isDeviceConflict = responseCode == 401 || responseCode == 403 || responseCode == 458
        val s = _state.value
        val live = s.isLive

        // Device-conflict: server keeps the old session alive for several
        // seconds after ExoPlayer released it. Immediate retry => another 403.
        if (isDeviceConflict) {
            if (s.autoReconnectCount < 2) {
                val waitMs = 5000L * (s.autoReconnectCount + 1)
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "تعارض اتصال — إعادة المحاولة بعد ${waitMs / 1000} ثانية...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(waitMs)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = it.autoReconnectCount + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
                return
            }
            if (live && s.formatAttempt + 1 < repo.liveExtensions().size) {
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "جارٍ تبديل الصيغة بعد 5 ثوانٍ...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(5000)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = 0,
                        formatAttempt = it.formatAttempt + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
                return
            }
            _state.update { it.copy(
                reconnecting = false,
                error = "تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى للأجهزة المتصلة أو انتهى الاشتراك ($responseCode)",
            ) }
            return
        }

        // Network / other errors — short backoff
        when {
            s.autoReconnectCount < 2 -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "جارٍ إعادة الاتصال بعد 2 ثانية...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(2000)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = it.autoReconnectCount + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
            }
            live && s.formatAttempt + 1 < repo.liveExtensions().size -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(
                    reconnecting = true,
                    error = "جارٍ تبديل الصيغة بعد 3 ثوانٍ...",
                ) }
                retryJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                    delay(3000)
                    _state.update { it.copy(
                        autoReconnectCount = 0,
                        formatAttempt = it.formatAttempt + 1,
                        error = null,
                    ) }
                }
            }
            else -> {
                val hint = when (responseCode) {
                    404 -> "القناة أو المحتوى غير متوفر حالياً على السيرفر (404)"
                    500, 502, 503 -> "خلل في خادم البث ($responseCode)"
                    else -> if (errorCodeName.isNotBlank()) "فشل تشغيل البث ($errorCodeName)" else Ar.errPlayback
                }
                _state.update { it.copy(reconnecting = false, error = hint) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun streamUrl(item: PlayItem, formatAttempt: Int): String = repo.streamUrl(item, formatAttempt)

    /**
     * LIVE-01: called by [LiveVideoStallWatchdog] after a repeated silent video-decoder stall.
     * Switches to the next live container (m3u8 <-> ts) if one is available.
     * Returns true when a switch was scheduled (the prepare effect will reload the stream).
     */
    fun switchLiveFormatAfterStall(): Boolean {
        val s = _state.value
        if (!s.isLive) return false
        val formats = repo.liveExtensions().size
        if (formats < 2) return false
        retryJob?.cancel()
        _state.update {
            it.copy(
                formatAttempt = (it.formatAttempt + 1) % formats,
                autoReconnectCount = 0,
                reconnecting = false,
                error = null,
            )
        }
        return true
    }

    suspend fun resolveResumePositionMs(item: PlayItem, currentPlayerPositionMs: Long): Long {
        val s = _state.value
        val resume = when {
            item.kind == Kind.LIVE || initialRequest.fromStart || forceFromZeroId == item.id -> 0L
            s.autoReconnectCount > 0 && currentPlayerPositionMs > 0L -> currentPlayerPositionMs
            initialRequest.initialPositionMs > 0L && !usedInitialPos &&
                item.id == initialRequest.items.getOrNull(initialRequest.startIndex)?.id -> {
                usedInitialPos = true
                initialRequest.initialPositionMs
            }
            else -> container.prefs.positionOf(item.kind, item.id)
        }
        if (forceFromZeroId == item.id) forceFromZeroId = null
        return resume.coerceAtLeast(0L)
    }

    fun onPlaybackEnded(totalDurationMs: Long, onNoNextItem: () -> Unit) {
        val s = _state.value
        if (s.isLive) return
        if (totalDurationMs > 0L) {
            container.saveProgress(s.currentItem, totalDurationMs, totalDurationMs)
        }
        val nextInPlaylist = if (s.index < s.playlist.lastIndex) s.playlist[s.index + 1] else null
        if (nextInPlaylist != null) {
            forceFromZeroId = nextInPlaylist.id
            goTo(s.index + 1, totalDurationMs, totalDurationMs)
            return
        }
        val allEps = s.seriesDetails?.episodes.orEmpty()
        val curEpIdx = allEps.indexOfFirst { it.id == s.currentItem.id }
        val nextEp = if (curEpIdx >= 0 && curEpIdx + 1 < allEps.size) allEps[curEpIdx + 1] else null
        if (nextEp != null) {
            forceFromZeroId = nextEp.id
            playEpisodeFromDrawer(nextEp, totalDurationMs, totalDurationMs)
        } else {
            onNoNextItem()
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/player/SahabPlaybackService.kt ===
package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import androidx.annotation.OptIn
import androidx.media3.common.Player
import androidx.media3.common.util.UnstableApi
import androidx.media3.session.MediaSession
import androidx.media3.session.MediaSessionService
import com.saydlawy.sahab.MainActivity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow

/**
 * O-03: Coordinates the active [Player] with [SahabPlaybackService] (background live audio)
 * and phone Picture-in-Picture state without affecting Android TV behavior.
 */
object SahabMediaSessionBridge {
    @Volatile
    var activePlayer: Player? = null
        private set

    @Volatile
    var pipAllowed: Boolean = false

    val inPipMode = MutableStateFlow(false)

    fun attach(context: Context, player: Player, allowBackgroundService: Boolean) {
        activePlayer = player
        if (allowBackgroundService) {
            runCatching {
                context.applicationContext.startService(
                    Intent(context.applicationContext, SahabPlaybackService::class.java),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun detach(context: Context, player: Player) {
        if (activePlayer === player) {
            activePlayer = null
            pipAllowed = false
            inPipMode.value = false
            runCatching {
                context.applicationContext.stopService(
                    Intent(context.applicationContext, SahabPlaybackService::class.java),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
class SahabPlaybackService : MediaSessionService() {

    private var mediaSession: MediaSession? = null

    private fun buildSession(player: Player): MediaSession {
        val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
        }
        val sessionActivity = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            0,
            intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        return MediaSession.Builder(this, player)
            .setSessionActivity(sessionActivity)
            .build()
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        syncActiveSession()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        syncActiveSession()
        return super.onStartCommand(intent, flags, startId)
    }

    private fun syncActiveSession() {
        val player = SahabMediaSessionBridge.activePlayer ?: return
        val existing = mediaSession
        if (existing == null) {
            mediaSession = buildSession(player)
        } else if (existing.player !== player) {
            existing.player = player
        }
    }

    override fun onGetSession(controllerInfo: MediaSession.ControllerInfo): MediaSession? {
        if (!isAllowedMediaController(controllerInfo.uid, android.os.Process.myUid(), controllerInfo.isTrusted)) {
            return null
        }
        syncActiveSession()
        return mediaSession
    }

    internal companion object {
        fun isAllowedMediaController(uid: Int, myUid: Int, isTrusted: Boolean): Boolean =
            uid == myUid || isTrusted
    }

    override fun onTaskRemoved(rootIntent: Intent?) {
        val player = mediaSession?.player
        if (player == null || !player.playWhenReady) {
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        mediaSession?.release()
        mediaSession = null
        super.onDestroy()
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player

import android.content.Context
import androidx.annotation.OptIn
import androidx.media3.common.AudioAttributes
import androidx.media3.common.C
import androidx.media3.common.Format
import androidx.media3.common.MimeTypes
import androidx.media3.common.Tracks
import androidx.media3.common.util.UnstableApi
import androidx.media3.database.StandaloneDatabaseProvider
import androidx.media3.datasource.DataSource
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.CacheDataSource
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.CacheKeyFactory
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.LeastRecentlyUsedCacheEvictor
import androidx.media3.datasource.cache.SimpleCache
import androidx.media3.datasource.okhttp.OkHttpDataSource
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.DefaultLoadControl
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.DefaultRenderersFactory
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.ExoPlayer
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.source.DefaultMediaSourceFactory
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.trackselection.DefaultTrackSelector
import androidx.media3.exoplayer.upstream.DefaultLoadErrorHandlingPolicy
import androidx.media3.extractor.DefaultExtractorsFactory
import androidx.media3.extractor.ts.DefaultTsPayloadReaderFactory
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import java.io.File
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

/**
 * Shared Media3 (ExoPlayer) factory with:
 * - Singleton 250 MB [SimpleCache] on disk for VOD (Movies & Series episodes) so scrubbing/rewinding/reopening is instant.
 * - Ultra-fast startup buffer thresholds ([DefaultLoadControl]) for sub-second playback start.
 * - Hardware/Software decoder fallback & TS/HLS/DASH/RTSP support.
 */
@OptIn(UnstableApi::class)
object SahabPlayer {

    const val SEEK_STEP_MS = 10_000L
    private const val VIDEO_JOINING_TIME_MS = 3_000L
    private const val MEDIA_CACHE_BYTES = 512L * 1024L * 1024L // 512 MB

    @Volatile
    private var simpleCache: SimpleCache? = null

    @Synchronized
    private fun getMediaCache(context: Context): SimpleCache? {
        simpleCache?.let { return it }
        return runCatching {
            val appContext = context.applicationContext
            val dir = File(appContext.cacheDir, MEDIA_CACHE_DIR).apply { mkdirs() }
            val evictor = LeastRecentlyUsedCacheEvictor(MEDIA_CACHE_BYTES)
            val dbProvider = StandaloneDatabaseProvider(appContext)
            SimpleCache(dir, evictor, dbProvider).also { simpleCache = it }
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun clearMediaCache(context: Context) {
        runCatching { simpleCache?.release() }
        simpleCache = null
        runCatching {
            File(context.applicationContext.cacheDir, MEDIA_CACHE_DIR).deleteRecursively()
        }
    }

    private const val MEDIA_CACHE_DIR = "sahab_media_cache"
    private val CACHE_KEY_CREDENTIAL_REGEX = Regex("/(live|movie|series)/[^/]+/[^/]+/")

    internal val leaseCoordinator = PlayerLeaseCoordinator()
    private val playerHandles = java.util.Collections.synchronizedMap(
        java.util.IdentityHashMap<ExoPlayer, ExoPlayerLeaseHandle>(),
    )

    private class ExoPlayerLeaseHandle(
        private val player: ExoPlayer,
        override val role: PlayerRole,
    ) : LeasedPlayerHandle {
        @Volatile
        var onRevoked: (() -> Unit)? = null

        override fun stopAndRelease() {
            playerHandles.remove(player)
            onRevoked?.invoke()
            onRevoked = null
            runCatching { player.stop() }
            runCatching { player.clearMediaItems() }
            runCatching { player.release() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Binds an optional callback invoked when [player] is released or preempted by another player lease.
     */
    fun bindOnRelease(player: ExoPlayer, onReleased: () -> Unit) {
        playerHandles[player]?.onRevoked = onReleased
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops, clears, and releases [player] once via [leaseCoordinator].
     */
    fun releasePlayer(player: ExoPlayer?) {
        if (player == null) return
        val handle = playerHandles.remove(player)
        if (handle != null) {
            leaseCoordinator.release(handle)
        } else {
            runCatching { player.stop() }
            runCatching { player.clearMediaItems() }
            runCatching { player.release() }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Synchronously stops and releases any active preview player before fullscreen playback begins.
     */
    fun releasePreviewPlayers() {
        leaseCoordinator.releaseByRole(PlayerRole.PREVIEW)
    }

    fun warmUpMediaCache(context: Context) {
        getMediaCache(context)
    }

    fun create(
        context: Context,
        client: OkHttpClient,
        isLive: Boolean,
        isPreview: Boolean = false,
        accountHash: String = "anon",
    ): ExoPlayer {
        // Release any existing active player lease before allocating a new ExoPlayer/MediaCodec instance.
        leaseCoordinator.releaseAll()
        val renderers = DefaultRenderersFactory(context)
            .setEnableDecoderFallback(true)
            .setExtensionRendererMode(DefaultRenderersFactory.EXTENSION_RENDERER_MODE_PREFER)
            // LIVE-01: let video join the already-running audio clock without dropping the first frames.
            .setAllowedVideoJoiningTimeMs(VIDEO_JOINING_TIME_MS)

        // LIVE-01: adapt across any quality / fps / codec / audio format the provider sends.
        val trackSelector = DefaultTrackSelector(
            context,
            DefaultTrackSelector.Parameters.Builder(context)
                .setAllowVideoMixedMimeTypeAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowVideoNonSeamlessAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowAudioMixedMimeTypeAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowAudioMixedSampleRateAdaptiveness(true)
                .setAllowAudioMixedChannelCountAdaptiveness(true)
                .setExceedVideoConstraintsIfNecessary(true)
                .setExceedRendererCapabilitiesIfNecessary(true)
                .build(),
        )

        val extractors = DefaultExtractorsFactory()
            .setConstantBitrateSeekingEnabled(true)
            .setTsExtractorFlags(
                DefaultTsPayloadReaderFactory.FLAG_ALLOW_NON_IDR_KEYFRAMES or
                    DefaultTsPayloadReaderFactory.FLAG_DETECT_ACCESS_UNITS,
            )

        // LIVE-01: live segments/playlists must never go through the shared 50 MB OkHttp disk cache
        // (stale .m3u8 + disk write latency). newBuilder() keeps the shared connection pool/dispatcher.
        val httpClient = if (isLive) client.newBuilder().cache(null).build() else client
        val httpFactory = OkHttpDataSource.Factory(httpClient)
            .setUserAgent("Sahab/1.0 (Android)")

        val dataSourceFactory: DataSource.Factory = if (!isLive) {
            val cache = getMediaCache(context)
            if (cache != null) {
                val sanitizedKeyFactory = CacheKeyFactory { dataSpec ->
                    val rawKey = dataSpec.key ?: dataSpec.uri.toString()
                    // Isolate cache per account: /kind/<accountHash>/<id>.<ext>
                    // (hash derived from server+user; never stores credentials in cache keys)
                    rawKey.replace(CACHE_KEY_CREDENTIAL_REGEX, "/$1/$accountHash/")
                }
                CacheDataSource.Factory()
                    .setCache(cache)
                    .setCacheKeyFactory(sanitizedKeyFactory)
                    .setUpstreamDataSourceFactory(httpFactory)
                    .setFlags(CacheDataSource.FLAG_IGNORE_CACHE_ON_ERROR)
            } else {
                httpFactory
            }
        } else {
            httpFactory
        }

        val sources = DefaultMediaSourceFactory(context, extractors)
            .setDataSourceFactory(dataSourceFactory)
            .setLoadErrorHandlingPolicy(DefaultLoadErrorHandlingPolicy(if (isLive) 4 else 3))
            .apply {
                if (isLive) {
                    // LIVE-01: never speed up / slow down live playback to chase the live edge.
                    // Speed changes skew the AudioTrack clock and trigger video drop-frame storms.
                    setLiveMinSpeed(1f)
                    setLiveMaxSpeed(1f)
                }
            }

        val loadControl = when {
            isPreview -> DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
                .setBufferDurationsMs(
                    /* minBufferMs = */ 4_000,
                    /* maxBufferMs = */ 12_000,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackMs = */ 450,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackAfterRebufferMs = */ 1_000,
                )
                .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
                .build()
            isLive -> DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
                .setBufferDurationsMs(
                    /* minBufferMs = */ 10_000,
                    /* maxBufferMs = */ 30_000,
                    // LIVE-01: fast start but with enough reorder headroom for 50/60fps HEVC/FHD/4K.
                    /* bufferForPlaybackMs = */ 1_000,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackAfterRebufferMs = */ 2_500,
                )
                .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
                .setBackBuffer(0, false)
                .build()
            else -> DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
                .setBufferDurationsMs(
                    /* minBufferMs = */ 20_000,
                    /* maxBufferMs = */ 60_000,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackMs = */ 800,
                    /* bufferForPlaybackAfterRebufferMs = */ 1_800,
                )
                .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
                .build()
        }

        return ExoPlayer.Builder(context, renderers)
            .setTrackSelector(trackSelector)
            .setMediaSourceFactory(sources)
            .setLoadControl(loadControl)
            .setAudioAttributes(AudioAttributes.DEFAULT, !isPreview)
            .setHandleAudioBecomingNoisy(!isPreview)
            .setWakeMode(C.WAKE_MODE_NETWORK)
            .setSeekBackIncrementMs(SEEK_STEP_MS)
            .setSeekForwardIncrementMs(SEEK_STEP_MS)
            .build()
            .also { created ->
                val role = if (isPreview) PlayerRole.PREVIEW else PlayerRole.FULLSCREEN
                val handle = ExoPlayerLeaseHandle(created, role)
                playerHandles[created] = handle
                leaseCoordinator.acquire(handle)
            }
    }

    /** Formats a live technical video badge from active tracks. */
    fun videoBadge(tracks: Tracks, fallbackQuality: String? = null): String {
        val selectedFormat = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { group ->
                (0 until group.length).firstOrNull { group.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { group.getTrackFormat(it) }
                    ?: if (group.length > 0) group.getTrackFormat(0) else null
            }

        if (selectedFormat != null && selectedFormat.height > 0) {
            val h = selectedFormat.height
            val res = when {
                h >= 2160 -> "4K"
                h >= 1440 -> "2K"
                h >= 1080 -> "1080p"
                h >= 720 -> "720p"
                h >= 540 -> "540p"
                else -> "${h}p"
            }
            val fps = if (selectedFormat.frameRate > 0f) {
                " ${selectedFormat.frameRate.roundToInt()}fps"
            } else ""
            return "$res$fps"
        }
        return fallbackQuality?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "HD"
    }

    /** Formats an audio stream badge from active tracks. */
    fun audioBadge(tracks: Tracks): String? {
        val selectedFormat = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_AUDIO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { group ->
                (0 until group.length).firstOrNull { group.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { group.getTrackFormat(it) }
                    ?: if (group.length > 0) group.getTrackFormat(0) else null
            } ?: return null

        val langCode = selectedFormat.language
        val langName = if (!langCode.isNullOrBlank() && langCode != "und") {
            Locale.forLanguageTag(langCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")).ifBlank { langCode }
        } else {
            selectedFormat.label?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "الصوت"
        }

        val codec = codecShortName(selectedFormat)
        return if (codec != null) "$langName ($codec)" else langName
    }

    fun videoQualityLabel(format: Format): String {
        val h = format.height
        val res = when {
            h >= 2160 -> "4K (${h}p)"
            h >= 1080 -> "FHD (${h}p)"
            h >= 720 -> "HD (${h}p)"
            h > 0 -> "SD (${h}p)"
            else -> format.label ?: "مسار فيديو"
        }
        val fps = if (format.frameRate > 0f) " · ${format.frameRate.roundToInt()}fps" else ""
        val bitrate = if (format.bitrate > 0) " · ${String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", format.bitrate / 1_000_000f)} Mbps" else ""
        return "$res$fps$bitrate"
    }

    fun activeBitrateText(tracks: Tracks, isLive: Boolean): String {
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g -> (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) } }
        if (format != null && format.bitrate > 0) {
            return String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f Mbps", format.bitrate / 1_000_000f)
        }
        return ""
    }

    fun activeAudioLabel(tracks: Tracks): String {
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_AUDIO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g ->
                (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) }
                    ?: if (g.length > 0) g.getTrackFormat(0) else null
            } ?: return "الصوت الافتراضي"

        val langCode = format.language
        val langName = if (!langCode.isNullOrBlank() && langCode != "und") {
            Locale.forLanguageTag(langCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")).ifBlank { langCode }
        } else {
            format.label?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "الصوت"
        }
        val codec = codecShortName(format)
        return if (codec != null) "$langName ($codec)" else langName
    }

    fun activeSubtitleLabel(tracks: Tracks, disabled: Boolean): String {
        if (disabled) return "بدون ترجمة"
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_TEXT && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g -> (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) } }
            ?: return "بدون ترجمة"
        val langCode = format.language
        val langName = if (!langCode.isNullOrBlank() && langCode != "und") {
            Locale.forLanguageTag(langCode).getDisplayLanguage(Locale.forLanguageTag("ar")).ifBlank { langCode }
        } else {
            format.label?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "ترجمة"
        }
        return "$langName (CC)"
    }

    fun activeQualityButtonLabel(tracks: Tracks, hasOverride: Boolean, isLive: Boolean): String {
        val format = tracks.groups
            .firstOrNull { it.type == C.TRACK_TYPE_VIDEO && it.isSelected }
            ?.let { g -> (0 until g.length).firstOrNull { g.isTrackSelected(it) }?.let { g.getTrackFormat(it) } }
        val h = format?.height ?: 0
        val res = when {
            h >= 2160 -> "4K"
            h >= 1080 -> "1080p FHD"
            h >= 720 -> "720p HD"
            h > 0 -> "${h}p"
            else -> ""
        }
        return when {
            res.isBlank() -> if (hasOverride) "الجودة" else "تلقائي"
            hasOverride -> res
            else -> "تلقائي $res"
        }
    }

    private fun codecShortName(format: Format): String? {
        val mime = format.sampleMimeType ?: return null
        return when (mime) {
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_AAC -> "AAC"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_AC3 -> "AC3"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_E_AC3, MimeTypes.AUDIO_E_AC3_JOC -> "EAC3"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_MPEG -> "MP3"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_OPUS -> "OPUS"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_VORBIS -> "Vorbis"
            MimeTypes.AUDIO_FLAC -> "FLAC"
            else -> mime.substringAfter('/', "").uppercase(Locale.US).takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        }
    }
}
