@Composable
internal fun CloudAccountPanel(
    email: String?,
    onLogin: () -> Unit,
    onLogout: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val container = LocalContainer.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var currentPinConfigured by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pinInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var savingPin by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(email) {
        currentPinConfigured = !container.prefs.getCloudSyncPin().isNullOrEmpty()
    }

    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)) {
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(
                        Modifier
                            .size(44.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                            .background(Palette.Blue.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                            .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    ) {
                        Icon(Ic.Cloud, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text("الحساب السحابي", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                        Text(
                            if (email != null) "متصل" else "غير متصل",
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            color = if (email != null) Palette.Emerald else Palette.Gold,
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (email != null) Tag("Cloud", Palette.Blue)
            }
            if (email != null) {
                InfoRow("البريد الإلكتروني", email)
                InfoRow(
                    "تشفير الاشتراكات (Sync PIN)",
                    if (currentPinConfigured) "مفعّل (PBKDF2-GCM)" else "محلي فقط (Android Keystore)",
                )
                Text(
                    text = if (currentPinConfigured) {
                        "تُشفَّر بيانات الاشتراك على السحابة برمز المزامنة الخاص بك؛ أدخله عند الاستعادة على جهاز جديد."
                    } else {
                        "بدون رمز مزامنة (Sync PIN)، تبقى بيانات الاشتراك مشفرة محلياً عبر Android Keystore ولا تُرفع للسحابة."
                    },
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                )
                GlassTextField(
                    value = pinInput,
                    onValueChange = { pinInput = it },
                    placeholder = "رمز حماية المزامنة (4+ أحرف أو أرقام)",
                    keyboardType = androidx.compose.ui.text.input.KeyboardType.Password,
                    password = true,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = if (savingPin) "جاري الحفظ..." else "حفظ الرمز ومزامنة",
                        primary = true,
                        onClick = {
                            if (savingPin) return@GlassButton
                            val trimmed = pinInput.trim()
                            if (trimmed.length < 4) {
                                Toast.makeText(context, "أدخل 4 أحرف/أرقام على الأقل", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                return@GlassButton
                            }
                            savingPin = true
                            scope.launch {
                                container.prefs.setCloudSyncPin(trimmed)
                                val accounts = container.prefs.getAccounts()
                                if (accounts.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    container.userSyncService.saveAccounts(accounts, pinOverride = trimmed)
                                }
                                currentPinConfigured = true
                                pinInput = ""
                                savingPin = false
                                Toast.makeText(context, "تم تفعيل تشفير المزامنة برمز الحماية", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    if (currentPinConfigured) {
                        GlassButton(
                            text = "إلغاء الرمز",
                            onClick = {
                                scope.launch {
                                    container.prefs.setCloudSyncPin(null)
                                    currentPinConfigured = false
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "تم التحويل للتخزين المحلي فقط (Keystore)", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
                GlassButton(
                    text = "تسجيل الخروج من السحابة",
                    onClick = onLogout,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_cloud_logout_btn"),
                    icon = Ic.Close,
                )
            } else {
                Text("لم يتم تسجيل الدخول، بياناتك تحفظ محلياً على الجهاز.", fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                GlassButton(
                    text = "تسجيل الدخول السحابي",
                    onClick = onLogin,
                    primary = true,
                    icon = Ic.Cloud,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_cloud_login_btn"),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun AccountPanel(
    account: Account?,
    info: UserInfo?,
    onSyncNow: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .size(44.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                        .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Person, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text("اشتراك المشاهدة (Xtream)", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                    Text(account?.user.orEmpty().ifBlank { "-" }, fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.Glow)
                }
            }
            val activeStatus = info?.status.orEmpty().ifBlank { "Active" }
            Tag(activeStatus, Palette.Emerald)
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
        InfoRow("الخادم", hostOf(account?.server.orEmpty()))
        InfoRow("اسم المستخدم", account?.user.orEmpty())
        if (info != null) {
            InfoRow("الحالة", if (info.status.isBlank()) "Active" else info.status)
            val expiry = info.expiry
            InfoRow("ينتهي الاشتراك", if (expiry == null || expiry <= 0L) Ar.subscriptionOpenShort else formatDate(expiry))
            if (info.maxConnections.isNotBlank()) {
                InfoRow("الأجهزة المتصلة", "${info.activeConnections.ifBlank { "0" }} / ${info.maxConnections}")
            }
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
        GlassButton(
            text = "تحديث ومزامنة قوائم القنوات والأفلام الآن",
            onClick = onSyncNow,
            icon = Ic.Refresh,
            primary = true,
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_sync_now_btn"),
        )
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun AiKeysPanel(aiKeys: ApiKeyManager, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    val keys by aiKeys.keys.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())
    val activeId by aiKeys.activeId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = "")
    var showAdd by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var newKeyText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var newKeyLabel by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            DotTitle("مفاتيح Gemini", Palette.Glow, fontSize = 16.sp)
            Text("أضف مفاتيح لتوليد قصص الأفلام باللغة العربية عند الحاجة.", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
            if (!aiKeys.isEncrypted) {
                Text(
                    "تنبيه: جهازك لا يدعم التشفير الآمن للمفاتيح، تم الاحتفاظ بها في الذاكرة المؤقتة فقط ولن تُحفظ بنص صريح على القرص.",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Palette.Gold,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.12f))
                        .padding(10.dp),
                )
            }
            keys.forEach { key ->
                Row(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                        .background(if (key.id == activeId) Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.20f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.04f))
                        .padding(12.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(key.label, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                        Text(key.value.take(8) + "…" + key.value.takeLast(4), fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                    }
                    if (key.id == activeId) {
                        Tag("نشط", Palette.Emerald)
                    } else {
                        GlassButton(text = "تفعيل", onClick = { aiKeys.setActive(key.id) })
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    GlassButton(text = "حذف", onClick = { aiKeys.removeKey(key.id) })
                }
            }
            GlassButton(
                text = "+ إضافة مفتاح",
                onClick = {
                    focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
                    showAdd = true
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn"),
                primary = true,
            )
        }
    }

    if (showAdd) {
        Dialog(
            onDismissRequest = {
                showAdd = false
                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
            },
        ) {
            GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 22.dp) {
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .imePadding(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                ) {
                    Text("مفتاح Gemini جديد", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Palette.Text)
                    GlassTextField(value = newKeyLabel, onValueChange = { newKeyLabel = it }, placeholder = "اسم مختصر (اختياري)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                    GlassTextField(value = newKeyText, onValueChange = { newKeyText = it }, placeholder = "المفتاح (AIza...)", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                        GlassButton(
                            text = "إضافة",
                            onClick = {
                                if (aiKeys.addKey(newKeyText, newKeyLabel) != null) {
                                    newKeyText = ""
                                    newKeyLabel = ""
                                    showAdd = false
                                    focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
                                }
                            },
                            primary = true,
                        )
                        GlassButton(
                            text = "إلغاء",
                            onClick = {
                                showAdd = false
                                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_add_ai_key_btn")
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun SettingsAppearancePanel(container: AppContainer) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val prefs = container.prefs
    val saved by prefs.themePreferences.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = ThemePreferences())
    val moviesLayout by prefs.moviesLayoutStyle.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = VodLayoutStyle.CLASSIC)
    val seriesLayout by prefs.seriesLayoutStyle.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = VodLayoutStyle.CLASSIC)
    val light by prefs.lightEffects.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = false)

    val showRecents by prefs.showRecents.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val showLiveChannels by prefs.showLiveChannelsOnHome.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val showRecommended by prefs.showRecommendedOnHome.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val showQuickCategories by prefs.showQuickCategoriesOnHome.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val homeChannelsLayout by prefs.homeChannelsLayout.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = HomeChannelsLayout.CAROUSEL)

    val activeThemeId = saved.selectedThemeId
    val customization = saved.customization(activeThemeId)

    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)) {
        VodLayoutSelectorPanel(
            title = "تخطيط شاشة الأفلام",
            subtitle = "اختر التصميم الخاص بقسم الأفلام بشكل مستقل",
            selected = moviesLayout,
            keyPrefix = "movies_layout",
            onSelect = { style -> scope.launch { prefs.setMoviesLayoutStyle(style) } },
        )

        VodLayoutSelectorPanel(
            title = "تخطيط شاشة المسلسلات",
            subtitle = "اختر التصميم الخاص بقسم المسلسلات بشكل مستقل",
            selected = seriesLayout,
            keyPrefix = "series_layout",
            onSelect = { style -> scope.launch { prefs.setSeriesLayoutStyle(style) } },
        )

        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                DotTitle("ثيم التطبيق", Palette.Glow, fontSize = 18.sp)
                themeOptions.forEach { opt ->
                    val isSelected = activeThemeId == opt.id
                    GlassCard(
                        onClick = { scope.launch { prefs.setSelectedTheme(opt.id) } },
                        selected = isSelected,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_theme_${opt.id}"),
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        ) {
                            Column(Modifier.weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
                                Text(opt.title, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                Text(opt.subtitle, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 12.sp)
                            }
                            if (isSelected) Tag("مطبق", Palette.Emerald)
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (activeThemeId == "oled_cinema") {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    DotTitle("لون التركيز في سينما OLED", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 15.sp)
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .focusGroup(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                    ) {
                        OledAccentPresets.forEach { preset ->
                            val selectedAccent = saved.oledAccentPreset == preset.id
                            GlassCard(
                                onClick = { scope.launch { prefs.setOledAccentPreset(preset.id) } },
                                selected = selectedAccent,
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_oled_accent_${preset.id}"),
                            ) {
                                Column(
                                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 10.dp, horizontal = 4.dp),
                                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                                ) {
                                    Box(
                                        Modifier
                                            .size(22.dp)
                                            .clip(CircleShape)
                                            .background(preset.accent)
                                            .border(1.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.5f), CircleShape),
                                    )
                                    Text(
                                        text = preset.nameAr,
                                        color = if (selectedAccent) Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                DotTitle("الخط والزجاج", Palette.Royal, fontSize = 17.sp)
                AppearanceChoiceRow(
                    title = "نوع الخط",
                    options = listOf("cairo" to "القاهرة", "sans" to "بسيط", "serif" to "نسخي", "mono" to "أحادي"),
                    selected = customization.fontFamily,
                ) { font ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(fontFamily = font) } }
                }
                AppearanceSlider(
                    title = "حجم الخط",
                    value = customization.fontScale,
                    range = 0.8f..1.35f,
                    label = "${(customization.fontScale * 100).toInt()}٪",
                ) { scale ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(fontScale = scale) } }
                }
                AppearanceSlider(
                    title = "استدارة البطاقات",
                    value = customization.cornerRadius,
                    range = 0f..36f,
                    label = "${customization.cornerRadius.toInt()} dp",
                ) { radius ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(cornerRadius = radius) } }
                }
                AppearanceSlider(
                    title = "شفافية الزجاج",
                    value = customization.glassOpacity,
                    range = 0.2f..1f,
                    label = "${(customization.glassOpacity * 100).toInt()}٪",
                ) { opacity ->
                    scope.launch { prefs.updateThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) { it.copy(glassOpacity = opacity) } }
                }
                var resetFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                Text(
                    text = "إعادة ضبط إعدادات هذا الثيم",
                    color = if (resetFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.Glow,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                        .onFocusChanged { resetFocused = it.isFocused }
                        .then(
                            if (resetFocused) Modifier.border(1.5.dp, Palette.FocusCyan, RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                            else Modifier,
                        )
                        .clickable { scope.launch { prefs.resetThemeCustomization(activeThemeId) } }
                        .padding(vertical = 8.dp, horizontal = 10.dp),
                )
            }
        }

        AppearancePanel(
            light = light,
            onToggleLight = { scope.launch { prefs.setLightEffects(!light) } },
        )

        HomeCustomizationPanel(
            showRecents = showRecents,
            showLiveChannels = showLiveChannels,
            showRecommended = showRecommended,
            showQuickCategories = showQuickCategories,
            homeChannelsLayout = homeChannelsLayout,
            onToggleShowRecents = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowRecents(!showRecents) } },
            onToggleShowLive = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowLiveChannelsOnHome(!showLiveChannels) } },
            onToggleShowRecommended = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowRecommendedOnHome(!showRecommended) } },
            onToggleShowQuickCategories = { scope.launch { prefs.setShowQuickCategoriesOnHome(!showQuickCategories) } },
            onSelectLayout = { layout -> scope.launch { prefs.setHomeChannelsLayout(layout) } },
            onClearRecents = {
                scope.launch {
                    prefs.clearRecentsForAccount(prefs.currentActiveAccountId())
                }
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun VodLayoutSelectorPanel(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    selected: VodLayoutStyle,
    keyPrefix: String,
    onSelect: (VodLayoutStyle) -> Unit,
) {
    GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            DotTitle(title, Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 18.sp)
            Text(subtitle, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 12.sp)
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                VodLayoutStyle.entries.forEach { style ->
                    val active = selected == style
                    GlassCard(
                        onClick = { onSelect(style) },
                        selected = active,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "${keyPrefix}_${style.id}"),
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        ) {
                            LayoutWireframePreview(style, active)
                            Row(
                                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            ) {
                                Text(style.labelAr, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold)
                                if (active) Tag("نشط", Palette.Emerald)
                            }
                            Text(style.descAr, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 11.sp, maxLines = 2)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LayoutWireframePreview(style: VodLayoutStyle, active: Boolean) {
    val barColor = if (active) Palette.FocusCyan.copy(alpha = 0.55f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f)
    val cardColor = if (active) Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.45f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f)
    Column(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(48.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.35f))
            .padding(6.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp),
    ) {
        when (style) {
            VodLayoutStyle.CLASSIC -> {
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp), modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Box(Modifier.width(18.dp).height(34.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(barColor))
                    Row(Modifier.weight(1f), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                        repeat(3) {
                            Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(34.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(cardColor))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            VodLayoutStyle.PRIME -> {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(16.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(barColor))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                    repeat(4) {
                        Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(14.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(cardColor))
                    }
                }
            }
            VodLayoutStyle.NETFLIX -> {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(18.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(barColor))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                    repeat(5) {
                        Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(12.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(cardColor))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AppearanceChoiceRow(
    title: String,
    options: List<Pair<String, String>>,
    selected: String,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
        Text(title, color = Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 13.sp)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.focusGroup(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        ) {
            options.forEach { (key, label) ->
                val active = selected == key
                var chipFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                Text(
                    text = label,
                    color = if (active || chipFocused) Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                        .background(
                            when {
                                active -> Palette.Royal
                                chipFocused -> Palette.CardSurfaceElevated
                                else -> Palette.CardSurface
                            },
                        )
                        .onFocusChanged { chipFocused = it.isFocused }
                        .border(
                            width = if (chipFocused) 2.dp else 1.dp,
                            color = if (chipFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Color.Transparent,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        )
                        .clickable { onSelect(key) }
                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AppearanceSlider(
    title: String,
    value: Float,
    range: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float>,
    label: String,
    onValue: (Float) -> Unit,
) {
    var localValue by remember(value) { mutableStateOf(value) }
    var sliderFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val step = ((range.endInclusive - range.start) / 20f).coerceAtLeast(0.02f)
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .onFocusChanged { sliderFocused = it.isFocused }
            .border(
                width = if (sliderFocused) 1.5.dp else 0.dp,
                color = if (sliderFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Color.Transparent,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
            )
            .padding(horizontal = if (sliderFocused) 8.dp else 0.dp, vertical = if (sliderFocused) 4.dp else 0.dp),
    ) {
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween) {
            Text(
                title,
                color = if (sliderFocused) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.TextDim,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
            )
            Text(label, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
        Slider(
            value = localValue.coerceIn(range.start, range.endInclusive),
            onValueChange = { localValue = it },
            onValueChangeFinished = { onValue(localValue) },
            valueRange = range,
            modifier = Modifier.onPreviewKeyEvent { event ->
                if (event.type == KeyEventType.KeyDown) {
                    when (event.nativeKeyEvent.keyCode) {
                        android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_RIGHT -> {
                            val next = (localValue + step).coerceIn(range.start, range.endInclusive)
                            localValue = next
                            onValue(next)
                            true
                        }
                        android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_LEFT -> {
                            val next = (localValue - step).coerceIn(range.start, range.endInclusive)
                            localValue = next
                            onValue(next)
                            true
                        }
                        else -> false
                    }
                } else {
                    false
                }
            },
        )
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.settings

import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement

import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape

import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.saydlawy.sahab.AppContainer

import com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.components.GlassButton

import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

@Composable
internal fun SettingsCustomTvPanel(
    container: AppContainer,
    homeChannelsSource: HomeChannelsSource,
    homeChannelsCategoryId: String,
    homeCustomChannelIds: Set<String>,
) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val dao = container.channelDb.channelDao()
    val activeAccountId by container.prefs.activeAccountId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(
        initialValue = container.prefs.currentActiveAccountId(),
    )
    val customLists by remember(dao, activeAccountId) {
        dao.observeLists(activeAccountId)
    }.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())
    val hiddenIds by dao.observeHiddenIds().collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())

    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)) {
        HomeChannelsSelectionPanel(
            container = container,
            source = homeChannelsSource,
            selectedCategoryId = homeChannelsCategoryId,
            customChannelIds = homeCustomChannelIds,
            onSelectSource = { src -> scope.launch { container.prefs.setHomeChannelsSource(src) } },
            onSelectCategory = { catId ->
                scope.launch {
                    container.prefs.setHomeChannelsCategoryId(catId)
                    container.prefs.setHomeChannelsSource(HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY)
                }
            },
            onToggleCustomChannel = { chId ->
                scope.launch {
                    container.prefs.toggleHomeCustomChannel(chId)
                    container.prefs.setHomeChannelsSource(HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM)
                }
            },
            onClearCustomChannels = {
                scope.launch { container.prefs.setHomeCustomChannelIds(emptySet()) }
            },
        )

        GlassPanel(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                DotTitle("قوائم البث المخصصة والقنوات المخفية", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 18.sp)
                Text(
                    text = "يمكنك إنشاء وتعديل القوائم وترتيب القنوات وإخفائها مباشرة من شاشة البث المباشر.",
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                )

                InfoRow("عدد القوائم المخصصة", "${customLists.size}")
                InfoRow("عدد القنوات المخفية", "${hiddenIds.size}")

                if (customLists.isNotEmpty()) {
                    customLists.forEach { list ->
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.05f))
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        ) {
                            Text(list.name, color = Palette.Text, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            GlassButton(
                                text = "حذف القائمة",
                                onClick = {
                                    scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                        dao.clearListItems(list.listId)
                                        dao.deleteList(list.listId)
                                        withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "تم حذف قائمة ${list.name}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        }
                                    }
                                },
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (hiddenIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "إظهار جميع القنوات المخفية (${hiddenIds.size})",
                        primary = true,
                        onClick = {
                            scope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                dao.unhideAll(System.currentTimeMillis())
                                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "تمت استعادة جميع القنوات المخفية", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/saydlawy/sahab/ui/settings/SettingsHomeChannelsPanel.kt ===

@Composable
internal fun HomeChannelsSelectionPanel(
    container: AppContainer,
    source: HomeChannelsSource,
    selectedCategoryId: String,
    customChannelIds: Set<String>,
    onSelectSource: (HomeChannelsSource) -> Unit,
    onSelectCategory: (String) -> Unit,
    onToggleCustomChannel: (String) -> Unit,
    onClearCustomChannels: () -> Unit,
) {
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    var liveCategories by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Category>>(emptyList()) }
    var allChannels by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Channel>>(emptyList()) }
    var showChannelPickerDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(container.repo.account) {
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            runCatching {
                liveCategories = container.repo.liveCategories()
                allChannels = container.repo.peekAllLiveChannels() ?: container.repo.liveChannels(null)
            }
        }
    }

    GlassPanel(shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .size(44.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                        .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Tv, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text(
                        "اختيار قنوات الشاشة الرئيسية",
                        fontSize = 18.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                        color = Palette.Text,
                    )
                    Text(
                        "حدد مصدر القنوات التي تظهر في شريط القنوات المباشرة بالصفحة الرئيسية",
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = Palette.TextDim,
                    )
                }
            }

            // Option 1: AUTO
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "اختيار ذكي تلقائي (الافتراضي)",
                subtitle = "يعرض أشهر القنوات العربية والرياضية والإخبارية تلقائياً",
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.AUTO,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_auto",
                onClick = { onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.AUTO) },
            )

            // Option 2: FAVORITES
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "قنواتي المفضلة فقط",
                subtitle = "يعرض القنوات المباشرة التي قمت بإضافتها إلى المفضلة",
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.FAVORITES,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_favs",
                onClick = { onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.FAVORITES) },
            )

            // Option 3: CATEGORY
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "باقة محددة من السيرفر",
                subtitle = if (selectedCategoryId.isNotBlank()) {
                    val catName = liveCategories.firstOrNull { it.id == selectedCategoryId }?.name ?: selectedCategoryId
                    "الباقة المختارة: $catName"
                } else {
                    "اختر باقة كاملة (مثل beIN أو MBC أو OSN) لعرض قنواتها في الرئيسية"
                },
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_category",
                onClick = { onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY) },
            )

            if (source == HomeChannelsSource.CATEGORY && liveCategories.isNotEmpty()) {
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.04f))
                        .padding(12.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                ) {
                    DotTitle("اختر الباقة المفضلة للرئيسية:", Palette.FocusCyan, fontSize = 14.sp)
                    liveCategories.take(25).chunked(2).forEach { pair ->
                        Row(
                            Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        ) {
                            pair.forEach { cat ->
                                val isCatSelected = cat.id == selectedCategoryId
                                GlassCard(
                                    onClick = { onSelectCategory(cat.id) },
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_cat_pick_${cat.id}"),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                    selected = isCatSelected,
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = cat.name,
                                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                                        fontWeight = if (isCatSelected) FontWeight.ExtraBold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                        color = if (isCatSelected) Color.White else Palette.TextDim,
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            if (pair.size == 1) Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Option 4: CUSTOM
            SourceOptionCard(
                title = "تحديد قنوات مخصصة يدوياً (${customChannelIds.size} قناة مختارة)",
                subtitle = "اختر القنوات التي تريدها بالاسم واحدة تلو الأخرى لوضعها في الصفحة الرئيسية",
                selected = source == HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM,
                itemKey = "settings_ch_source_custom",
                onClick = {
                    onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM)
                    focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_ch_source_custom")
                    showChannelPickerDialog = true
                },
            )

            Row(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
            ) {
                GlassButton(
                    text = "تحديد القنوات يدوياً (${customChannelIds.size})",
                    onClick = {
                        onSelectSource(HomeChannelsSource.CUSTOM)
                        focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_open_ch_picker_btn")
                        showChannelPickerDialog = true
                    },
                    primary = true,
                    icon = Ic.Tv,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_open_ch_picker_btn"),
                )
                if (customChannelIds.isNotEmpty()) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "مسح الاختيار",
                        onClick = onClearCustomChannels,
                        modifier = Modifier.restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_clear_custom_ch_btn"),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showChannelPickerDialog) {
        CustomChannelPickerDialog(
            allChannels = allChannels,
            selectedIds = customChannelIds,
            onToggleChannel = onToggleCustomChannel,
            onDismiss = {
                showChannelPickerDialog = false
                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_open_ch_picker_btn")
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun CustomChannelPickerDialog(
    allChannels: List<Channel>,
    selectedIds: Set<String>,
    onToggleChannel: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    var query by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var filteredChannels by remember { mutableStateOf<List<Channel>>(emptyList()) }

    LaunchedEffect(allChannels, query, selectedIds) {
        if (query.isNotBlank()) {
            delay(180L)
        }
        val result = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val needle = query.normalizeForSearch().trim()
            if (needle.isBlank()) {
                val selectedFirst = allChannels.filter { it.id in selectedIds }
                val others = allChannels.filter { it.id !in selectedIds }.take(150)
                selectedFirst + others
            } else {
                allChannels.asSequence()
                    .filter { it.name.normalizeForSearch().contains(needle) }
                    .take(150)
                    .toList()
            }
        }
        filteredChannels = result
    }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .heightIn(max = 520.dp),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            strong = true,
            padding = 18.dp,
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
            ) {
                Row(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            "اختر قنوات الشاشة الرئيسية",
                            fontSize = 17.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                            color = Color.White,
                        )
                        Text(
                            "تم تحديد ${selectedIds.size} قناة",
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            color = Palette.FocusCyan,
                        )
                    }
                    GlassButton(
                        text = "تم",
                        onClick = onDismiss,
                        primary = true,
                    )
                }

                SearchBox(
                    value = query,
                    onValueChange = { query = it },
                    placeholder = "ابحث عن قناة (مثل: beIN, MBC, الجزيرة، روتانا)...",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )

                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(vertical = 4.dp),
                ) {
                    itemsIndexed(filteredChannels, key = { idx, ch -> "${ch.id}_$idx" }) { _, ch ->
                        val checked = ch.id in selectedIds
                        val logoUrl = remember(ch.icon, ch.name) {
                            ChannelLogoResolver.resolvePrimaryUrl(ch.icon, ch.name)
                        }
                        GlassCard(
                            onClick = { onToggleChannel(ch.id) },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                            selected = checked,
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                            ) {
                                Checkbox(
                                    checked = checked,
                                    onCheckedChange = null,
                                    colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(
                                        checkedColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                                        uncheckedColor = Palette.TextDim,
                                        checkmarkColor = Palette.CinematicBg,
                                    ),
                                )
                                Box(
                                    Modifier
                                        .size(34.dp)
                                        .clip(CircleShape)
                                        .background(Color.White)
                                        .padding(4.dp),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                                ) {
                                    if (logoUrl.isNotBlank()) {
                                        AsyncImage(
                                            model = logoUrl,
                                            contentDescription = ch.name,
                                            contentScale = ContentScale.Fit,
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        Text(
                                            text = ch.name.take(3),
                                            fontSize = 9.sp,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = Palette.CinematicBg,
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                                Text(
                                    text = ch.name,
                                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                                    fontWeight = if (checked) FontWeight.ExtraBold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun SourceOptionCard(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    itemKey: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, itemKey),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        selected = selected,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            RadioButton(
                selected = selected,
                onClick = null,
                colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(
                    selectedColor = Palette.FocusCyan,
                    unselectedColor = Palette.TextDim,
                ),
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    title,
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = if (selected) Color.White else Palette.Text,
                )
                Text(
                    subtitle,
                    fontSize = 11.5.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun HomeCustomizationPanel(
    showRecents: Boolean,
    showLiveChannels: Boolean,
    showRecommended: Boolean,
    showQuickCategories: Boolean,
    homeChannelsLayout: HomeChannelsLayout,
    onToggleShowRecents: () -> Unit,
    onToggleShowLive: () -> Unit,
    onToggleShowRecommended: () -> Unit,
    onToggleShowQuickCategories: () -> Unit,
    onSelectLayout: (HomeChannelsLayout) -> Unit,
    onClearRecents: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    GlassPanel(modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp), strong = true, padding = 20.dp) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .size(44.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                        .border(1.dp, Palette.Glow.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                ) {
                    Icon(Ic.Settings, null, tint = Palette.Glow, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                Column {
                    Text("التحكم في أقسام الشاشة الرئيسية", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, color = Palette.Text)
                    Text("إظهار أو إخفاء أي قسم وتخصيص طريقة العرض", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                }
            }

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض قسم تابع المشاهدة",
                subtitle = "إظهار شريط استكمال مشاهدة الأفلام والمسلسلات والقنوات",
                checked = showRecents,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_recents",
                onToggle = onToggleShowRecents,
            )

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض قسم القنوات المباشرة",
                subtitle = "إظهار شريط القنوات المباشرة المميزة في الصفحة الرئيسية",
                checked = showLiveChannels,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_live_channels",
                onToggle = onToggleShowLive,
            )

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض قسم أفلام مقترحة لك (أحدث الإضافات)",
                subtitle = "إظهار أحدث الأفلام المضافة من سيرفر Xtream وقاعدة بيانات Firebase",
                checked = showRecommended,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_recommended",
                onToggle = onToggleShowRecommended,
            )

            SettingsSwitchRow(
                title = "عرض بطاقات التصفح السريع",
                subtitle = "إظهار بطاقات البث المباشر — الأفلام — المسلسلات",
                checked = showQuickCategories,
                itemKey = "settings_toggle_quick_cats",
                onToggle = onToggleShowQuickCategories,
            )

            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
            DotTitle("طريقة عرض القنوات المباشرة في الرئيسية", Palette.Royal, fontSize = 15.sp)
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                val isCarousel = homeChannelsLayout == HomeChannelsLayout.CAROUSEL
                GlassCard(
                    onClick = { onSelectLayout(HomeChannelsLayout.CAROUSEL) },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_layout_carousel"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    selected = isCarousel,
                ) {
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isCarousel,
                            onClick = null,
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Palette.Glow, unselectedColor = Palette.TextDim),
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text("سلايدر أفقي", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isCarousel) Palette.Text else Palette.TextDim)
                            Text("شريط متحرك", fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                        }
                    }
                }
                val isGrid = homeChannelsLayout == HomeChannelsLayout.GRID
                GlassCard(
                    onClick = { onSelectLayout(HomeChannelsLayout.GRID) },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_layout_grid"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                    selected = isGrid,
                ) {
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isGrid,
                            onClick = null,
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(selectedColor = Palette.Glow, unselectedColor = Palette.TextDim),
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Column {
                            Text("شبكة", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (isGrid) Palette.Text else Palette.TextDim)
                            Text("صفوف متعددة", fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
            GlassButton(
                text = "مسح سجل المشاهدة بالكامل",
                onClick = onClearRecents,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_clear_recents_btn"),
            )
            val cacheCtx = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext.current
            val cacheScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
            var cacheCleared by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
            GlassButton(
                text = if (cacheCleared) "تم مسح كاش الصور والفيديو" else "مسح كاش الصور والفيديو",
                onClick = {
                    cacheScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.player.SahabPlayer.clearMediaCache(cacheCtx)
                        runCatching { java.io.File(cacheCtx.cacheDir, "image_cache").deleteRecursively() }
                        withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { cacheCleared = true }
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_clear_media_cache_btn"),
            )
            val weatherSvc = com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.LocalContainer.current.weather
            var cityInput by remember { mutableStateOf(weatherSvc.getManualCity()) }
            var cityStatus by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                GlassTextField(
                    value = cityInput,
                    onValueChange = { cityInput = it; cityStatus = null },
                    placeholder = "مدينة الطقس (اتركه فارغاً للتلقائي)",
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                )
                GlassButton(
                    text = "حفظ",
                    onClick = {
                        cacheScope.launch {
                            val ok = weatherSvc.setManualCity(cityInput)
                            cityStatus = if (ok) {
                                if (cityInput.isBlank()) "تم التفعيل التلقائي" else "تم تثبيت المدينة"
                            } else "لم يتم العثور على المدينة"
                        }
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_save_weather_city_btn"),
                )
            }
            cityStatus?.let { st ->
                Text(st, fontSize = 11.sp, color = Palette.Gold)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun SettingsSwitchRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    itemKey: String,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
) {
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onToggle,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {
                toggleableState = ToggleableState(checked)
                contentDescription = "$title، $subtitle"
            }
            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, itemKey),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        selected = checked,
        role = Role.Switch,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                DotTitle(title, if (checked) Palette.FocusCyan else Palette.TextDim, fontSize = 14.sp)
                Spacer(Modifier.height(3.dp))
                Text(subtitle, fontSize = 11.5.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
            }
            Switch(
                checked = checked,
                onCheckedChange = null,
                colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                    checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                    checkedTrackColor = Palette.Royal,
                    uncheckedThumbColor = Palette.TextDim,
                    uncheckedTrackColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f),
                ),
            )
        }
    }
}

package com.saydlawy.sahab.ui.settings

@Composable
fun SettingsScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onLoggedOut: () -> Unit,
    onOpenCloudLogin: () -> Unit = {},
) {
    val container = LocalContainer.current
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val displayMode = LocalDisplayMode.current
    val focusNav = LocalFocusNavigation.current
    val isWide = displayMode == DisplayMode.TABLET_OR_TV

    val savedCategory = focusNav.getSelectedCategory(Routes.SETTINGS)
    var selectedCategory by remember(isWide) {
        mutableStateOf<String?>(savedCategory ?: if (isWide) "appearance" else null)
    }
    var showLogoutConfirm by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val selectCat: (String?) -> Unit = { cat ->
        selectedCategory = cat
        if (cat != null) {
            focusNav.setSelectedCategory(Routes.SETTINGS, cat)
        }
    }

    BackHandler(enabled = !isWide && selectedCategory != null) {
        val prevCat = selectedCategory
        selectedCategory = null
        if (prevCat != null) {
            focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_menu_$prevCat")
        }
    }

    BackHandler(enabled = isWide) {
        val activeKey = "settings_menu_${selectedCategory ?: "appearance"}"
        focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, activeKey)
        onBack()
    }

    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        if (isWide || selectedCategory == null) {
            SahabTopBar(current = Routes.SETTINGS, onBack = onBack)
        }

        if (isWide) {
            Row(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(22.dp),
            ) {
                // Right column in RTL: Settings Sidebar
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(0.32f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .focusGroup()
                        .focusRestorer()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .padding(bottom = 12.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                ) {
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المظهر وتصميم الشاشات",
                        subtitle = "تخطيط الأفلام، المسلسلات، الثيمات، والأداء",
                        icon = Ic.Settings,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "appearance",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_appearance",
                        isDefault = true,
                        onClick = { selectCat("appearance") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "قوائم البث والتخصيص",
                        subtitle = "قنوات الرئيسية، القوائم المخصصة، والمخفية",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "custom_tv",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_custom_tv",
                        onClick = { selectCat("custom_tv") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المصادر والاشتراكات",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مصادر Xtream / M3U وتحديث الاشتراك",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "sources",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_sources",
                        onClick = { selectCat("sources") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الحساب السحابي والمزامنة",
                        subtitle = "تسجيل الدخول، رمز التشفير، والمزامنة",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "cloud",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_cloud",
                        onClick = { selectCat("cloud") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الذكاء الاصطناعي (Gemini)",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مفاتيح توليد قصص الأفلام",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = selectedCategory == "ai",
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_ai",
                        onClick = { selectCat("ai") },
                    )

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.logout,
                        onClick = {
                            focusNav.markNavigateFrom(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_logout_btn")
                            showLogoutConfirm = true
                        },
                        icon = Ic.Close,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_logout_btn"),
                    )
                }

                // Left column in RTL: Active Settings Panel
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(0.68f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .focusGroup()
                        .focusRestorer()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                ) {
                    SettingsDetailContent(selectedCategory ?: "appearance", container, onOpenCloudLogin, onLoggedOut)
                }
            }
        } else {
            if (selectedCategory == null) {
                Column(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
                ) {
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المظهر وتصميم الشاشات",
                        subtitle = "تخطيط الأفلام، المسلسلات، الثيمات، والأداء",
                        icon = Ic.Settings,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_appearance",
                        onClick = { selectCat("appearance") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "قوائم البث والتخصيص",
                        subtitle = "قنوات الرئيسية، القوائم المخصصة، والمخفية",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_custom_tv",
                        onClick = { selectCat("custom_tv") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "المصادر والاشتراكات",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مصادر Xtream / M3U وتحديث الاشتراك",
                        icon = Ic.Tv,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_sources",
                        onClick = { selectCat("sources") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الحساب السحابي والمزامنة",
                        subtitle = "تسجيل الدخول، رمز التشفير، والمزامنة",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_cloud",
                        onClick = { selectCat("cloud") },
                    )
                    SettingsMenuItem(
                        title = "الذكاء الاصطناعي (Gemini)",
                        subtitle = "إدارة مفاتيح توليد قصص الأفلام",
                        icon = Ic.Cloud,
                        isSelected = false,
                        itemKey = "settings_menu_ai",
                        onClick = { selectCat("ai") },
                    )

                    Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.logout,
                        onClick = { showLogoutConfirm = true },
                        icon = Ic.Close,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    )

                    Text(
                        text = Ar.version(appVersion(context).ifBlank { "1.0" }),
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        color = Palette.TextDim,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                    )
                }
            } else {
                Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    SahabTopBar(
                        current = Routes.SETTINGS,
                        onBack = {
                            val prev = selectedCategory
                            selectedCategory = null
                            if (prev != null) {
                                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_menu_$prev")
                            }
                        },
                    )
                    Column(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                            .padding(16.dp),
                    ) {
                        SettingsDetailContent(selectedCategory, container, onOpenCloudLogin, onLoggedOut)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showLogoutConfirm) {
        LogoutConfirmationDialog(
            onConfirm = {
                showLogoutConfirm = false
                onLoggedOut()
            },
            onDismiss = {
                showLogoutConfirm = false
                focusNav.restoreAfterDialogDismiss(Routes.SETTINGS, "settings_logout_btn")
            },
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LogoutConfirmationDialog(
    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
) {
    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        GlassPanel(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
            strong = true,
            padding = 22.dp,
        ) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                Text(Ar.logoutQuestion, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, color = Palette.Text)
                Text(Ar.logoutHint, fontSize = 13.sp, color = Palette.TextDim)
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.logout,
                        onClick = onConfirm,
                        primary = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    GlassButton(
                        text = Ar.cancel,
                        onClick = onDismiss,
                        autoFocus = true,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsMenuItem(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    icon: ImageVector,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    itemKey: String,
    isDefault: Boolean = false,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    var focused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .restorableFocus(Routes.SETTINGS, itemKey, isDefaultEntry = isDefault)
            .onFocusChanged { focused = it.isFocused },
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
        selected = isSelected || focused,
    ) {
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .size(44.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                    .background(if (isSelected || focused) Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.4f) else Palette.Royal.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                    .border(1.dp, Palette.FocusCyan.copy(alpha = 0.35f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
            ) {
                Icon(icon, null, tint = Palette.FocusCyan, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(title, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold, color = Palette.Text)
                Text(
                    subtitle,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = Palette.TextDim,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
            }
            Text("<", fontSize = 20.sp, color = Palette.Glow, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsDetailContent(
    category: String?,
    container: AppContainer,
    onOpenCloudLogin: () -> Unit,
    onLoggedOut: () -> Unit,
) {
    val cloudUser = container.authManager.currentUser
    val account: Account? = container.repo.account
    val info: UserInfo? = container.repo.userInfo

    val homeChannelsSource by container.prefs.homeChannelsSource.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = HomeChannelsSource.AUTO)
    val homeChannelsCategoryId by container.prefs.homeChannelsCategoryId.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = "")
    val homeCustomChannelIds by container.prefs.homeCustomChannelIds.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptySet())

    when (category) {
        "appearance", "customize", "themes" -> SettingsAppearancePanel(container = container)
        "custom_tv", "home_channels" -> SettingsCustomTvPanel(
            container = container,
            homeChannelsSource = homeChannelsSource,
            homeChannelsCategoryId = homeChannelsCategoryId,
            homeCustomChannelIds = homeCustomChannelIds,
        )
        "cloud" -> {
            CloudAccountPanel(
                email = cloudUser?.email,
                onLogin = onOpenCloudLogin,
                onLogout = {
                    container.authManager.signOut()
                    onLoggedOut()
                }
            )
        }
        "sources" -> {
            val appCtx = LocalContext.current.applicationContext
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)) {
                SourcesPanel(container = container)
                ActiveSourceDetailsPanel(
                    container = container,
                    account = account,
                    info = info,
                    onSyncNow = {
                        container.syncCoordinator.triggerManualSync(appCtx)
                    }
                )
            }
        }
        "ai" -> {
            AiKeysPanel(container.aiKeys)
        }
    }
}
